Let us also give due respect to every *guru,* GBC member, temple president, and rank-and-file member of this wonderful ISKCON movement for pushing on your mission, being enthused by you to do so, through thick and thin, friend and foe. I would be honored to put the feet of any of these great Vaiṣṇavas on my head and serve them until my very last breath.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving our lives meaning. Thank you for teaching us how to revive our dormant love for Kṛṣṇa. Our debt is indeed unrepayable. I hope to take birth again and again in this movement, to have an opportunity to go to your *gurukula* and associate with all these wonderful devotees, who are indeed oceans of good qualities. My only prayer is that I may never fall away from the association of your ISKCON devotees.

Your servants in Dallas, Texas.  
(written by Jitavrata dāsa, the servant of your servants)

## Denver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this auspicious day.

You are the life of our life, and we dedicate our thoughts, words, and deeds to you. Please keep us always in the association of your servants so that together we may be effective in assisting you in your mission with whatever insignificant abilities we may individually possess.

“Now all my disciples must work combinedly and with cooperation to spread this *saṅkīrtana* movement. If you cannot work together, then my work is stopped up. Our Society is like one big family, and our relationships should be based on love and trust. We must give up the fighting spirit and use our intelligence to push ahead. You should accept help from your Godbrothers.” (letter from Śrīla Prabhupāda to Upendra, August, 6, 1970)

Here in Denver, the heads of each department meet weekly to sort out any problems which may arise, as well as to plan the strategy for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness; they see to it that each member of the community is fully engaged in devotional service to the best of his or her capabilities. Indeed, what other reason is there for residing in a polluted city except for the tremendous facility it affords to expose hundreds of thousands of people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Many different avenues are being utilized for the fulfillment of this one purpose.

Denver and the neighboring cities provide numerous locations for distributing your books. Also, there are regular *hari-nāma* parties, college programs, *nāma-haṭṭa* programs, an annual Ratha-yātrā with the Festival of India, as well as participation in a big city parade. And there is lots of *prasādam* distribution.

“Yes, I fully agree that ‘What is the use of a temple if there is no *prasādam* distribution?’ *Prasādam* distribution on a large scale must be resumed. Such a temple where there is no such distribution has no value, I agree.” (letter from Śrīla Prabhupāda to Tamāl Krishna Goswami, January 11, 1974)

Over one hundred people daily attend Govinda’s Buffet, except on Sundays, when about 200 guests sit in rows in the temple room and are served a sumptuous feast. There is also a Food for Life program, which generates respect for ISKCON from the public as well as government officials.

Besides receiving *bhoga* offerings tirelessly prepared by the kitchen team, the exquisitely beautiful Deities—Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā—are looked after by caring *pūjārīs* who attend to the details of Their service. Who can fail to be captivated after once beholding Their beauty?

You are our professor, and to help us assimilate the transcendental knowledge in your books, various educational projects are maintained. Besides enlivening *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* classes, there are additional classes for *bhaktas* and *bhaktins,* as well as retreats. VIHE seminars have also been implemented. So that the children may also experience the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as well as your protection, there is a *gurukula* for both primary and secondary education, a nursery school, a Sunday school, and a youth forum for teen-agers.

“One who hears the transcendental sound vibration of the holy name of the Lord, Hare Kṛṣṇa, also improves in health. We have actually seen that many *brahmacārīs* and *gṛhasthas* connected with the International Society for Krishna Consciousness have improved in health, and a luster has come to their faces. It is essential that a *brahmacārī* engaged in spiritual advancement look very healthy and lustrous.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.21.47, purport)

As we engage in chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa together in the morning, even our mental illness is mitigated. To maintain optimum health, the devotees are provided with a kind of health insurance that various naturopathic practitioners participate in.

In July of 1975, you visited here for six days. As a Centennial offering to you, a committee is working together to create a “Śrīla Prabhupāda Library and Museum” in the quarters where you stayed. Your *mūrti* will be installed and the quarters will be restored to the condition they were in while you were here. There will also be a full library of your books, microfilm, videos, photographs, and a database of all your recorded words. We are confident that your presence will easily be perceived by everyone who visits.

You are our guiding light and have given us the simple formula for becoming purified enough to gain your association.

“The *prajā-pati* is Lord Viṣṇu, and He is the Lord of all living creatures, all worlds, and all beauties, and the protector of everyone. The Lord created this material world to enable the conditioned souls to learn how to perform *yajñas* (sacrifices) for the satisfaction of Viṣṇu, so that while in the material world they can live very comfortably without anxiety and after finishing the present material body they can enter into the kingdom of God. That is the whole program for the conditioned soul.” (*Bhagavad-gītā* 3.10, purport)

Your eternal servants in Denver, Colorado.  
(written by Kamalinī-devī dāsī)

## Detroit

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, your disciples and devotees at Devasadan Mandir, offer our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Here in Detroit we have a long history in ISKCON. Under your guidance, the Detroit temple was opened in 1969. First as a house on Forest St., near Wayne State University. Then, when we outgrew that facility, the temple was moved to 8311 Jefferson St., in the city’s historic Indian Village.

In the summer of 1971, Your Divine Grace blessed the city of Detroit with your presence. Taking compassion upon all the residents here, you requested your disciples to go door to door and preach. To fulfill your, desire the city was divided into sections, and *hari-nāma* parties were sent out daily to different streets and avenues, until the entire city had had the opportunity to receive the blessing of the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Meanwhile, devotees with *Back to Godhead* magazines and *Kṛṣṇa* book would knock at each door. Detroit was a city of racial unrest in the 60’s, and to see blacks and whites chanting and dancing together was very much appreciated. Many times people would purchase your *Back to Godhead* magazine and with tears in their eyes ask if the devotees were from up above.

As your temple began to grow, in 1972 you approved the installation of large marble Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa deities to accompany the Jagannātha deities that were already being worshiped. You named Them Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī. They are one of the first sets of large marble Deities to be installed in America. To this day, they are still worshiped with six *āratis* and six offerings daily, every day of the year. Their clothes are changed twice daily and at least four new day outfits and night outfits are offered each year.

As your temple expanded, it became apparent that a new building would have to be purchased to accommodate all the new *bhaktas* and congregational members. You then graced the city of Detroit again with your merciful presence, once in August 1975 and later in June 1976. In 1975, your advice and approval was needed on the possible purchase of the lavish Fisher Mansion at 383 Lenox. You very much liked the building, which had a ballroom with a marble floor and a ceiling two stories high. The ballroom resembled the open courtyard in the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandir in Vṛndāvana. This room was perfectly suited for a temple. You inspired two of your disciples who had come from prominent Detroit families, Ambarīśa Prabhu and Lekhaśravantī dāsī, to purchase the building, teaching us that wealth, far from being an impediment to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, can be used to perform wonderful service for Kṛṣṇa.

During your visit in June 1976, you saw the installation of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, thus completing the three altars in the main temple room.

As your temple and congregation grew, many Indian devotees came forward to help your mission. First they took responsibility for the Sunday Feast program and made it most successful. Regularly reading your books, they took the lead role in printing *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in Gujarati. By your mercy only, the temple grew in popularity and the boat-well was converted into Śrīnāthjī Hall in record time. With the success of Śrīnāthjī Hall, most of the debt to the temple was paid off.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we don’t know what further wonderful plans you have for us here. Please purify our hearts and allow us to be instruments in your divine hands.

A few months ago, a former professor from Berkeley University in California came to Govinda’s restaurant for dinner. He told us that he had seen Your Divine Grace many times on his way to work back in 1969. He told us that the movement was very small then, and he had no idea that it would become as large as it is today. One of the devotees here took compassion upon this poor soul and offered him a volume of the *Prabhupāda Līlāmṛta,* suggesting he read it to learn about this personality whom he had regularly seen at that time. He graciously received the book and lamented his inability to appreciate your presence back in 1969.

Begging to remain your servants,  
Your devotees at Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan.  
(written by Jagad-guru dāsa)

## Durban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Attempts to realize your transcendental vision of a whole world of devotees are forging ahead. Your sincere devotees here in South Africa are active assistants in this colossal endeavor, whose success will be possible only by your divine mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, twenty years ago, in October 1975, your lotus feet graced the soil of South Africa. You visited Chatsworth and turned that place into a *tīrtha*. You desired a temple there, and ten years later your devotees presented you with the majestic Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānāth Temple of Understanding to house Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānāth and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Your temple—the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānāth Temple of Understanding, at the southernmost tip of Africa—has to date attracted hundreds of thousands of persons through its doors, to feast their eyes on the most exquisite forms of Their Lordships. Every festival meets with enormous success—each is of mammoth proportions, hosting thousands of people. And all these successes have served only as the run-up to celebrate the most auspicious event: the Centennial of the appearance of Your Divine Grace.

By your guidance, we have managed to attract many government dignitaries to visit your Temple. The highlight was the recent visit of President Nelson Mandela at a huge Diwali festival. He entered the Temple, bowed down before you, took *darśana* of Their Lordships, and honored *caraṇāmṛta*. Thus, through your extensive efforts, many leaders are beginning to appreciate the potency of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We are personally witnessing and experiencing your mercy through various preaching outlets. The Food for Life program is constantly increasing and is poised to fulfill its Centennial goals. The educational programs run by your Bhaktivedanta College of Education and Culture have expanded to other parts of the country and are attracting many students.

All our accomplishments and attempts at spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness are humbly submitted at your lotus feet. Please continue to glance mercifully upon your servants here in Durban.

Begging to remain engaged in your service,  
Your servants in Durban, South Africa.  
(written by Lokabandhu dāsa)

## Edmonton

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearmost, beloved *jagad-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda,

“Although we are unable to glorify you adequately, we nonetheless have a transcendental taste for glorifying your activities. We shall try to glorify you according to the instructions received from authoritative sages and scholars. Whatever we speak, however, is always inadequate and very insignificant.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.16.3)

I feel that this is our position, Śrīla Prabhupāda. How does an unqualified twelve-year-old begin to speak your inexhaustible glories? How do we start to pay a debt that can never be repaid? It cannot be done. Nonetheless, on behalf of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī Mandira in Edmonton, I shall endeavor to make a humble Vyāsa-pūjā offering.

One year before your Centennial anniversary, I look back over the year and see the great endeavors of the Edmonton devotees. This is the first time we have written a Vyāsa-pūjā offering, as we are a brand-new temple, joining your great ISKCON fleet in the ongoing battle against illusion. Exactly one year after its incorporation, the temple is planned to be opened on Janmāṣṭamī 1995. I see the smooth chain of events, from conception to construction. I see the ever-present struggles and the flourishing of the center, as we strive to give Edmontonians the sublime nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for which they are always hankering. And we always know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that it will all be accomplished only by your causeless mercy.

Who could bring the Divine Couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī, to Edmonton? None other than he who boarded the Jaladuta in 1965 at the age of sixty-nine, the embodiment of his spiritual master’s instructions, he who singlehandedly brought this magnificent movement to the very fallen souls of the Occident and who watched it flourish under the grace of Kṛṣṇa. Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

As this temple was built by you, it has also been built for you, as an offering for your most auspicious Centennial anniversary. Our first day of business is to be on your 1995 Vyāsa-pūjā day. We will continue to glorify you throughout your Centennial year, 1995-96, in your new center. We are another temple in the West, another soldier in your army. As one of your newest temples, we are hoping to continue your mission by following your instructions. May ISKCON continue to submerge the world in transcendental Kṛṣṇa consciousness. May Lord Caitanya’s mission survive and the holy name be chanted in every corner of the planet. May we remain loyal to you alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, always trying our best to serve your lotus feet, follow your instructions, and glorify you. And may you please continue showering your divine mercy upon all of us in Edmonton. Please accept our very humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!!

The devotees at ISKCON Edmonton, Canada.  
(written by Kriti Sharma, age 12)

## El Salvador

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

In this tiny and insignificant country, we have been trying to please you since 1978. Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Nityānanda Prabhu are so kind that They have been showering their causeless mercy since then. Of course, we can understand that the reason They have not departed from El Salvador is that They want to help you fulfill your deep desire to rescue all conditioned souls from this planet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we repay your causeless mercy?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we fulfill your pure desires?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we be totally surrendered to your mission?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we forget all our material designations and acquire real sincerity and humbleness for preaching effectively to accomplish the difficult tasks that lie ahead?

The promise that we are trying to cultivate in our hearts is to maintain ourselves as your faithful followers in order to fulfill your desire to propagate the glories of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. Although these two divine beings appeared in West Bengal five hundred years ago, They are now overflowing the hearts of all with sweet love of God.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we promise not to close this Yātrā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we promise to continue with enthusiasm.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we promise to work in cooperation with one another.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we promise to keep trying to please Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

But Śrīla Prabhupāda, only you can give us the strength to be able to fulfill all these promises. Otherwise we are lost!!

Your eternal servants at ISKCON El Salvador.

## Gītā-nāgarī

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine grace.

For all of your many disciples, granddisciples and followers, Vyāsa-pūjā is undoubtedly the most auspicious day of the year, in some ways even more glorious than the preceding day, Janmāṣṭamī. After all, Kṛṣṇa came and performed His pastimes, yet still I and many others continued cycling through the many species, with no opportunity for peace, happiness, and perfection. In addition, many powerful *ācāryas* came, Kṛṣṇa Himself even came again, in the guise of a devotee, and still I was wasting away as an aquatic, plant, insect, beast, human, and then back down again.

But your appearance, along with the movement you started, is responsible for saving so many lost souls, and your movement will continue and increase at least for 10,000 years. Therefore, out of great gratitude, and especially on this day, we want to glorify you, your activities, and your movement.

But how to best glorify you? Are flowery words of praise enough? It is a start, but Prahlāda Mahārāja gives an idea how to best show our gratitude.

He says:

*evaṁ janaṁ nipatitaṁ prabhavāhi-kūpe*  
*kāmābhikāmam anu yaḥ prapatan prasaṅgāt*  
*kṛtvātma-sāt surarṣiṇā bhagavan gṛhītaḥ*  
*so ’haṁ kathaṁ nu visṛje tava bhṛtya-sevām*

**"My dear Lord, O Supreme Personality of Godhead, because of my association with material desires, one after another, I was gradually falling into a blind well full of snakes, following the general populace. But your servant, Nārada Muni, kindly accepted me as his disciple and instructed me how to achieve this transcendental position.** ***Therefore my first duty is to serve him. How could I leave his service?*****” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **7.9.28)**

So this written homage is merely a token, a real and heartfelt token of our love and gratitude to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. But, the validity of our words and feelings will be proven by our dedication to your service.

Some service is common to all serious Prabhupādānugas. We all must chant the *mahā-mantra,* read your books, and worship the Deity. We all must help in some capacity to spread your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, and we all must try to attain love for Kṛṣṇa. Other services may be more individual, and here at Gītā-nāgarī we all have the common service of developing this project as you desire. Yet our endeavor is something like the Ganges worshiper offering Ganges water back to the Ganges River. Gītā-nāgarī was envisioned by you as far back as 1948 and started on your order in the mid seventies. It is to be developed only by faithfully following your instructions on self-sufficiency, cow protection, ox power, Vaiṣṇava association, and a myriad of other subjects. Like the Ganges worshiper, our only real contribution is to become devoted, energetic, and intelligent tools in your hands and to offer to you and Lord Kṛṣṇa that which is already yours—our very lives.

Then we can be assured of the success of Gītā-nāgarī *dhāma,* of our own lives, and of our endeavors to please you.

Begging to remain your menial servants,  
The residents of the Gītā-nāgarī farm, Pennsylvania.

## Govardhana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble, respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to your Divine Grace! All glories to your holy Appearance Day! All glories to the messages of Kṛṣṇa consciousness brought by you to deliver the whole world!

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on a forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master delivers the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is an ocean of auspicious qualities.”**

Ninety-nine years ago, by the will of Lord Kṛṣṇa, you descended from the spiritual abode to this world, a place full of hypocrisy and quarrels, to deliver the living entities afflicted by material duality.

*janasya kṛṣṇād vimukhasya daivād*  
*adharma-śīlasya su-duḥkhitasya*  
*anugrahāyeha caranti nūnaṁ*  
*bhūtāni bhavyāni janārdanasya*

**“O my Lord, great philanthropic souls travel on the earth on behalf of the Supreme Personality of Godhead to show compassion to the fallen souls who are averse to the sense of subordination to the Lord.”**

The wind may carry dust or fragrance, but the wind has nothing to do with these things. Similarly, you came to this world to revive the dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness within the hearts of the living entities. Your only purpose in coming here was to teach us by example how to love Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You have nothing to do with this mundane world. Your whole life is just like a light tower, guiding the ignorant living entities so that they can know how to live their lives and how to transcend birth and death.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, one who knows your activities can be inspired to go back home, back to Godhead. But how can we know you? When Lord Kṛṣṇa came, fools derided Him, not knowing His supreme nature. So what can ordinary men know of you? You are so humble. You did not manifest any mystic power, such as summoning the wind or rain or foretelling the future. Nor did you create any philosophy of your own. You only repeated the words of your spiritual master. Nevertheless, what you have given to this world is not at all ordinary. The books you compiled are many. The philosophy they contain is profound yet easily understood and readily accepted by all. You have established ISKCON, a huge organization. You have transformed so many fallen souls into pure devotees. All these acts are incredible.

We are unfortunate because we cannot physically associate with you and learn from you. For us, you are like the Lord, who seems to be both near and far away. Still, we can associate with you through your books, videos, and audio tapes, as well as through your dear son, Śrīla Gurudeva (Tamāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami). In this way we can relish a little bit of the ocean of nectar of your pastimes. We are dying of thirst for that nectar, though not being able to drink up the whole ocean, a drop of water is enough to give us unlimited joy. Spiritually speaking, we are all withering. Therefore, a drop of the nectar of your pastimes can make us feel completely happy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are as worshipable as the Lord, of whom you are the dearest servant. You kept spreading the glories and messages of the Lord while undergoing so many difficulties. So who can be dearer than you to the Lord?

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari (Kṛṣṇa).”**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Who would say that you have not come to us? You have. You are with us now and will be with us in the future. You are always with us. Yes, you are with us in the form of your books. Who would say that you are different from your books? Through your books, we can have the authoritative instructions of *guru, sādhu,* and *śāstra*. Who would say that you have left us and are no longer guiding us? You are still guiding, maintaining, and protecting us. You have constructed a house which is big enough to accommodate the whole world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to shower your mercy upon us and place us at your lotus feet.

*rahūgaṇaitat tapasā na yāti*  
*na cejyayā nirvapaṇād gṛhād vā*  
*na cchandasā naiva jalāgni-sūryair*  
*vinā mahat-pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekam*

**“My dear king Rahūgaṇa, unless one has the opportunity to smear his entire body with the dust of the lotus feet of great devotees, one cannot realize the Absolute Truth. One cannot realize the Absolute Truth simply by observing celibacy, strictly following the rules and regulations of householder life, leaving home as a** ***vānaprastha,*** **accepting** ***sannyāsa,*** **or undergoing severe penances by keeping oneself submerged in water in winter or surrounding oneself in summer by fire and the scorching heat of sun. There are many other processes to understand the Absolute Truth, but the Absolute Truth is revealed only to one who has attained the mercy of a great devotee.”**

However, in order to gain your mercy, we have to distribute your mercy, i.e., we have to distribute your books. The more we distribute your books, the more we will gain your mercy. The more mercy we gain, the more books we can distribute. In this way, the whole world will be filled up with your mercy and your books. We are praying that we can be a small wave in the torrents of this *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

Distributing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books *kī jaya*!  
Distributing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books *kī jaya*!  
Distributing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books *kī jaya*!

Your grandchildren in Govardhana, Khasadeśa.  
(written by Nitāi Caraṇa dāsa)

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Lord Caitanya’s glorious *saṅkīrtana* movement!

At this time of your Vyāsa-pūjā, I would humbly like to express my sentiments as well as those of other devotees on the Guyana *yātrā,* and I hope that this can be done properly.

Here in the ISKCON Guyana *yātrā* we share this one common understanding—we are eternally indebted to you, our dearmost grand–spiritual master for bringing us out from darkness into light. We are your dependents, and we implore you to give us your mercy, which we need for our spiritual growth. As we become more mature and realized, we can offer much more to you in service. I have heard some devotees express that they would like to do so much more for your Society but that it is a struggle to maintain some stability in their own spiritual lives. To gain your mercy, our desires have to be more sincere and intense, so please share your affection.

Through reading some of the books of my spiritual master, His Holiness Satsvarūpa Mahārāja, I can sense your immaculate status and better appreciate your glories, your contribution to humanity. It is such a glorious opportunity for all who are aspiring on the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to make a commitment to bring you into the focus of ISKCON as part of your Centennial celebration.

When we initiated our plans for your Centennial celebration, we became stagnated for awhile, but about a year ago we activated those plans and our preaching has come alive! Our Food for Life programs are touching the hearts of everyone, and now “Food for Life” has become a household name. Our *pāṇḍāl* programs are attracting the conditioned souls, and although people are plagued by their traditional concepts, they are inquiring into your explanations of Vedic philosophy, and this is leading them to our centers. The *pāṇḍāls* also inspire those who at some point wandered away from your shelter to take some steps back toward Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and our reunion committees are slowing bringing them back.

Of course, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the activities and plans for your glorification in the year of your Centennial and beyond once again constitute your plan to make us more dedicated in service to the Supreme Lord by glorifying your transcendental personality.

We may not make illustrious contributions comparable with those of our more celebrated Godbrothers and Godsisters elsewhere, but please continue to inspire us by blessing us with success in our endeavors.

Thanking you for kind reciprocation,  
Your servants in Guyana.  
(written by Haridāsa dāsa)

## Hardwar

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Caitanya Mahāprabhu writes in His *Śikṣāṣṭaka* (1):

*ceto-darpaṇa-mārjanaṁ bhava-mahā-dāvāgni-nirvāpaṇaṁ*  
*śreyaḥ-kairava-candrikā-vitaraṇaṁ vidyā-vadhū-jīvanam*  
*ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaṁ prati-padaṁ pūrṇāmṛtāsvādanaṁ*  
*sarvātma-snapanaṁ paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam*

**“Let there be all victory for the chanting of the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa, which can cleanse the mirror of the heart and stop the miseries of the blazing fire of material existence. That chanting is the waxing moon that spreads the white lotus of good fortune for all living entities. It is the life and soul of all education. The chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa expands the blissful ocean of transcendental life. It gives a cooling effect to everyone and enables one to taste full nectar at every step.”**

In this Kali-yuga, the *yuga-dharma* is the *saṅkīrtana-yajña* taught by Caitanya Mahāprabhu. But five hundred years ago the *saṅkīrtana-yajña* was confined to India alone. Prior to that, the Lord’s holy name had never been glorified outside India. Nevertheless, by the mercy of a pure Vaiṣṇava, the transcendental knowledge and messages have been spread all over the world. God actually can make this happen by Himself. However, He wanted to give credit to His pure devotee, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. God wanted to show us that only by the mercy of a pure devotee can one be delivered from the ocean of suffering and relish the nectar of devotion and love for Him.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we just want to please you because we want to achieve perfection. Our Guru Mahārāja, His Holiness Tamāl Krishna Goswami, said that though you have disappeared, you have never left us, for you are always in your books and instructions. You said, “If you want to please me, distribute my books!” By the mercy of our Guru Mahārāja, we have learned how to please you, i.e., to distribute your books. We are not qualified to serve Kṛṣṇa, but we have firm faith in your instructions. In this atheistic country of Khasadeśa, preaching is so difficult. But we will not compromise. Though it is not easy to move ahead, we believe that one day we can reach Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana. Regarding this, we are confident, so we will work harder and harder for this goal.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, all the ISKCON temples are celebrating your Centennial by offering you prayers. Khasadeśa is also fortunately participating by sending you Vyāsa-pūjā offerings for the first time. We hope that you will shower some special mercy upon us by sending more senior devotees here to give us their kind association. This is very crucial to the spreading of the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement in Khasadeśa. We are waiting for your mercy.

We also beg that by the grace of our Guru Mahārāja we will be able to remember your instructions always.

Your granddisciples in Hardwar, Khasadeśa.  
(written by Ajita dasa)

## Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Somehow, by your mercy, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness is still going strong. We have had many problems since your departure, but by your mercy ISKCON is still strong.

There are many sincere devotees in ISKCON who want to please you more than anything else; therefore ISKCON is still strong. I am very happy to be a part of your ISKCON, and happy to say that by your mercy and your sincere disciples, ISKCON is strong. I pray that I will always remain a part of your ISKCON, and become strong.

Your servants at the ISKCON center in Hartford, Connecticut.  
(written by Pyāri-mohana dāsa)

## Heidelberg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at the divine dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you and the *saṅkīrtana* mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

On this most glorious occasion of your appearance day, we want to thank you very much for all the mercy you are giving us constantly by residing in our temple and allowing us to worship you in some insignificant way.

Your presence is constantly felt by us. We know that you are residing in every atom of this ISKCON movement and that you are always busy in its affairs. You are guiding the sincere members of your movement from within their hearts and also through your teachings. When we look back at the past years in Germany, we note how by your grace things have been improving in the Kṛṣṇa conscious way. We have been trying to distribute your books profusely for the past twenty-six years, and this effort is still going on successfully here. In the last year we have also started a *prasādam*-distribution program. Our devotees are endeavoring tirelessly to give people a taste of the nectar for which everyone hankers. People are responding gratefully, sending us many nice letters. One such letter was written by a German lady who said, “Your sweet-balls are very delicious. They make one addicted to them.” Every one of us, of course, knows who brought the first *prasādam* sweet-balls to the West, and therefore all the credit for the distribution of mercy goes to you alone. Although we cannot perceive your physical presence with our materially contaminated eyes, still it is clear to us that your intense desire is the real strength behind all the activities of this movement.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very much inspired to serve you in this way and also to increase our *prasādam* distribution by means of our newly established Food for Life organization. We therefore pray for your blessings to be able to give out tons and tons of *prasādam*.

Please bless us to increase the distribution of your books, the only means for pacifying the disturbed minds of the conditioned souls suffering in the fearsome whirlpools of *māyā*.

We are confident that you are completely empowered to distribute the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu throughout the material world, so out of appreciation we want to surrender completely to your lotus feet and try to assist you by engaging in the *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Because you have given us the best thing, we don’t want to move even an inch away from your instructions or from what you have institutionalized.

As we approach the Centennial of your appearance day, we pray that we not become attracted to any position, ideology, or activity that can lead us away from the shelter of your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your sold-out men. Please give us your blessings.

Your servants in Heidelberg, Germany.

## Hong Kong

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Thank you for your mercy, which allows us to live in your instructions, which are like the sun of knowledge that gives us warmth and light and shows us the right path of life.

When we are performing our services and working under your instructions, we know that you are always with us, that we are living within your arms and that you are always protecting us.

You left us physically eighteen years ago, but still the influence of your thoughts and words is so deep that we strongly feel you are personally helping and guiding us in every aspects of our lives.

By your kindness, we now have a library with all your books—all your knowledge—a good start for your Centennial worship. We move under the instructions within your books, just as a puppet moves under the master's hands. We pray to Kṛṣṇa that we will be very careful to execute your instructions so that He will not take you away from us.

Within your transcendental visions, many spiritual farming communities were established all over the world. By your mercy, we also hope to establish such a Kṛṣṇa conscious farm as an offering to you.

We pray to have the same desire as yours, devoid of all personal motivation and false ego, and to tolerate the sufferings of mind and body in order to fulfill the mission of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

May your fame spread all over the three worlds!

Your devotees in Hong Kong.

## Houston

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most fallen humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Who can imagine the vision and mission of one so devoted to Lord Kṛṣṇa as you? Through your vision a small particle of dust has been cleared off our souls and we can understand our position, as part and parcel of Kṛṣṇa. Our only satisfaction is to ultimately serve him. There is no way to properly thank you and exclaim your glories except to humbly chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare and to feebly carry on your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your undeserving subservants in Houston, Texas.

## Israel

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

I was a young *brahmacārī* on the day of your disappearance from this mortal world. It was the day I was destined to leave my home temple and go on a mission to open a center in Israel. I felt it was your direct and last order to me and that I should take this order as my life and soul. Although I have no proper qualifications, in your order there is the potency to successfully follow it; therefore I am completely dependent on your causeless mercy. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, my lord, please bless our humble attempt to spread your mission.

After many years of struggle, it seems that a solid ground is being established, devotees are starting to join, and book distribution increases day by day.

All the devotees here are your humble servants, and they are very serious about dedicating their lives to saving the fallen souls.

By your mercy, even the most stubborn members of the dominant religion here are taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants in Israel.  
(written by Guṇa-avatāra dāsa)

## Karuṇa Bhavan

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, O master at whose feet all other masters bow. By your mercy a lame man can cross mountains, a blind man can see the stars in the sky, a dumb man can recite eloquent poetry, and the most fallen can return home back to Godhead.

When will that day come when we will be able to understand and appreciate your great mercy? We are sorry that we appear unable to comprehend your compassion and mercy. But without doubt we are the most fortunate recipients of that great mercy.

You were purported to have commented, “If I can send even one soul back to Godhead, then my mission will be successful.” Well, Śrīla Prabhupāda, without doubt your mission is very successful. Over the past year there have been a few very fortunate souls in your movement who have returned back home, back to Godhead. We here in your Scottish *yātrā* were, by your mercy, able to witness such a very wonderful event. By your mercy you delivered your dear daughter Ratnarañjinī-devī dāsī. We were able to witness her great transcendental transformation over a two-year period. By your sweet mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Ratnarañjinī gradually came to the point where she was not at all interested in anything around her in the least. She just continued to chant the holy names and listen to your *bhajan* and *kīrtana* tapes. She relished listening to your *Kṛṣṇa* book stories. But mostly she chanted and never showed any interest in anything else. By your wonderful mercy she became absorbed in the holy name. By you, working through this nice devotee of yours, you were teaching us all here in Scotland that we simply have to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and we will also return home, back to Godhead. You proved through this simple daughter of yours that the transcendental abode is easily attainable by simply following your instructions. She was not a *guru, sannyāsī,* GBC, or the like—she simply followed your instructions to the best of her ability, and for that endeavor you mercifully delivered her.

Now you have left us with a great lesson—the greatest lesson: just how to leave this material world and return to Kṛṣṇa. There is nothing very complicated about how to do it. As you have said, Śrīla Prabhupāda, “Kṛṣṇa consciousness is simple for the simple and complicated for the complicated.” Any problem lies in our application of your simple and straightforward instructions. We are sure that you must be so very fond of your devotees here, for you have given us such a wonderful example so early in our spiritual lives. We just have to absorb ourselves in the holy name and perform our service with devotion, determination, and attention, and you will surely deliver us all.

We would all like to offer our best possible appreciation for such a wonderful lesson. Surely such teachers as you are so very rare in the entire history of the universe. We do not know what could have caused you to show such mercy upon us fallen fools. But we are, by your causeless mercy, the most fortunate recipients. We hope that we have received it into our filthy hearts and that we will, from now on, be able to work towards finishing the duration of our lives in this world of suffering in the same way. We just want to keep calling out the wonderful name “Prabhupāda!” over and over again. We know that we are sentimentalists, but if we can be your sentimentalists—just very simple souls who chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, read your books, and perform our service with devotion and determination—that will be our perfection.

You gave your daughter to show us how it is done. Now we pray that she and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have not wasted any of your time and energy on us. We pray that we, by your mercy, can follow this wonderful example you have given us and that others present and in the future will also do the same.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! We offer our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet again and again. Thank you for such a wonderful example and such a special lesson. We pray never to forget how you have taught us, and we pray for your shelter and blessings so we may simply take shelter of the holy names, as you have shown. “The holy name alone can render all benedictions . . .” This you have most certainly proven to us.

We are sorry for all our offenses, sins, stupidity, and laziness. We hope that now we will be able to act, speak, hear, and think the way you want us to.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! You are certainly the most special devotee. Thank you!

We pray to allow ourselves to be delivered by your mercy also.

Your foolish but most fortunate admirers and aspiring servants at Karuṇa Bhavan, Scotland.

## Kaunas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s prediction that a great personality would come in the future to propagate Lord Caitanya’s movement throughout the whole world now has become a reality. You are that great personality. You have given us the chance to develop our love for Kṛṣṇa, and also the opportunity to join Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* party, to chant and dance and take *prasādam*.

Fifteen years ago on Sunday evenings, young people would gather in someone’s apartment in Kaunas and chant in ecstasy the name of Lord Gaurāṅga. They were the first followers of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in Lithuania.

Those of us who tasted even a little bit of Kṛṣṇa consciousness experienced transcendental bliss and were happy. The problems of material life diminished. What more can we desire? Live simply and think highly. Chant the holy name, and there will be no more problems. Just enjoy the spiritual nectar.

However, the advanced Vaiṣṇavas have a sense of great compassion for the suffering conditioned souls, and they are ready to overflood them with the mercy of the Lord, not taking into consideration all the troubles they have to overcome.

With the help of your devoted followers, you have also awakened this sense of compassion in our hearts and have given us the inspiration to think constantly about the means of spreading this great message to millions of people. By your mercy, even during the time of the atheistic regime we could find the ways to somehow publish, bind, and distribute your books, make programs and attract new people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Today we can see an increasing interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. So we beg your blessings for qualified preaching and reasonable work and full engagement in spreading this wonderful movement according to your desire.

Your insignificant servants in Kaunas, Lithuania.

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

A disciple once told you that if you had only written one book, *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* he would have been satisfied with that and considered it complete. You praised this attitude of the disciple—certainly not out of pride but because you knew that the essence of spiritual life is faith in the spiritual master. In fact, we make progress toward the ultimate goal of pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa only by your mercy, not otherwise.

Pleasing you is not impractical. Through faith in your instructions, anyone can become happy and fixed on the spiritual platform of service to Kṛṣṇa. This platform is beyond the mind and its speculations.

A true disciple is not distracted by anything. He does not have to consider his advancement separately. He only has to consider how to make the order of the spiritual master his life and soul. This is especially true for your followers. Lord Caitanya is Kṛṣṇa in the most merciful form, coming to give *kṛṣṇa-prema*. You are Lord Caitanya’s empowered representative, foretold by the scriptures and previous *ācāryas*. Therefore, you have also come to give *kṛṣṇa-prema*. Attachment to you, your example, and your instructions is in itself perfection. That perfection, for which we are always praying, will fulfill all desires.

You built a house in which the whole world can live. We see that this house is not made to encompass everyone by embracing so many relative philosophies and placing your instructions among them. This can only foster false ego and conflict. The foundation of this house is your instructions, as demonstrated by your living example. We cannot jump over you to other *ācāryas,* seeking to judge your position and teachings and adjust them to fit some new *siddhānta* born of our materially motivated speculations. If your teachings are seen to be relative truth and movable, then the house, having no foundation, will fall apart. Our consciousness will immediately become muddy and confused due to shifting our reference point from your absolute instructions to our flickering mind.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that we can become your true, faithful, humble, and fully surrendered followers. We want that perfection. We thank you for your many gifts, especially for the association of your sincere followers and the opportunity to serve them. Please grant us the benediction to be always absorbed in hearing and distributing your instructions. Especially for your Centennial, allow us to glorify you in a most wonderful way.

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Laguna Beach, California.

## Leicester

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As years go by, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can appreciate your mission, your greatness, and your transcendental qualities more and more. Where would we be now if you had not gone through all those difficulties to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West? Where would the world be now if you would not have left Vṛndāvana and taken Lord Caitanya’s message all around the globe?

In fact, you have brought this very precious Vṛndāvana *dhāma* with you and distributed it freely to everyone. You have taken this most valuable of all jewels and transplanted it right in our hearts. You are certainly the supreme magician, for no one else could have ever done something like this. You have completely changed our lives. In fact, you have changed everything in our lives, even the so-called small things in our day-to-day activities, and you have certainly changed the face of the earth. You have given our lives an entirely new direction, pointing us toward the ultimate goal of life, for which we were yearning throughout millions of lifetimes. This goal is not just something that will absorb us for five or ten years; it is the ultimate destination for which all living beings hanker birth after birth, namely, pure devotional service unto the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have presented Kṛṣṇa in such an attractive way that even our stonelike hearts have finally begun to melt. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have not just given us Kṛṣṇa and then left it all up to us to struggle along, but you have created a transcendental network of devotees and temples all around the world so that we can engage in devotional service and get purified. You have given us a mission in life: to take your compassion and distribute it freely to each and everyone we meet.

And above all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us these transcendental books of yours, your devotional ecstasies, which are indeed nondifferent from you and Lord Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can feel your presence in every single one of your words and every single one of your divine instructions. Nonetheless, we miss your physical presence very, very much on this most glorious of all occasions, your Vyāsa-pūjā day. Our only solace and shelter are our memories of you and your divine instructions, which are our life and soul.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have not even started to realize who you actually are. No one in the past and no one in the future will ever walk on the surface of the earth and do what you have done. You have brought Kṛṣṇa to the whole world and made Him available to all. You have fulfilled Lord Caitanya’s mission to distribute the holy name to every town and village, not only in India but throughout the whole world. Memories may fail and recordings will fade away, but these books of yours, dictated by the Supreme Lord Himself, will be there for a long, long time to come, at least for the next ten thousand years.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have also given us a responsibility, namely to maintain what you have created and to cooperate with one another. You have always been so open-minded and encouraging but at the same time strict and to the letter when it came to the Vaiṣṇava *siddhānta* coming down from the previous *ācāryas*. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us partake of your compassion at a time when impersonalism, factionalism, individualism, and noncooperation seem to be all-pervading. Help us to be strict with ourselves and full of compassion and understanding for others. In this way we will be able to build on this house in which the whole world can live together peacefully, side by side, glorifying Your Divine Grace and the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants in Leicester, UK.

## Liberia

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādād*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvāṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindan*

All glories unto your lotus feet!

Please accept our humble obeisances on this auspicious day of your appearance. O *guru* of the world, you are the embodiment of Lord Caitanya’s mercy. O servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, you are delivering this planet, which is filled with impersonalism, voidism, and illusion.

O Śrīla Prabhupada, your coming within the jurisdiction of the material world is not a condition forced by material nature, and this day offers us the greatest opportunity to express our feeling of appreciation and love for you.

We are simply begging that you be kind unto us and help us completely surrender and dedicate ourselves to your service. We have nothing to fear. In you we have found an anchor of life that keeps us secure in an ocean of miseries.

May your glories always be sung, and may you protect us from the disaster of giving up your instructions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your humble servants in ISKCON Liberia.  
(written by Śāstrīvara dāsa)

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Once more we have the opportunity to glorify you,  
So wonderful and strong in the service of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa,  
You set the perfect standard in everything you do,  
Every word you say brings us closer to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

To love you is not difficult—you are *bhakti* through and through,  
Your thoughts and your deeds are devoted to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa,  
Your presence brings all auspiciousness, your love is so true,  
You simply want to take us all back to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.

You speak with great compassion, your form has a golden hue,  
You are cleaning the three worlds with the Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa,  
Your preaching is ever fresh—just like the morning dew;  
Every heart you touch will chant Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa.

On introducing us to Kṛṣṇa—you told us He is blue;  
You reveal to us many beautiful forms of our sweet Lord Kṛṣṇa.  
Awakening dormant *bhakti*, you cleanse our hearts right through;  
You are the pure representative of our sweet Lord Kṛṣṇa.

You are a swanlike *paramahaṁsa*, so pure;  
Your books are infiltrating, bringing love of Kṛṣṇa.  
So many hardships for us you did endure;  
Your mission is the greatest of all—bringing love of Kṛṣṇa.

You tell of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes and the sweetness of His face.  
You said, “Your life will become sublime; simply chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.”  
You are our merciful savior; you are our Divine Grace.  
Please bless us to always remember the names and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.

You are the greatest revolutionary the world has ever seen;  
You are fearless, bold, and strong for your Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.  
This dirty world of darkness—only you can truly clean;  
You appeared in order to direct us back to your Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Please help us to hang on your lotus feet.

Your servants at ISKCON Liverpool, UK.

## Ljubljana

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana*! May the waves of your transcendental instructions for preaching and spreading this mission completely overflood us.

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā we will try, according to our realizations, to awaken even more our intense meditation on your unlimited and brilliant character.

We sometimes find it difficult to imagine that some day the whole world will be chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* but you were sure of this even before coming to the West. You had unflinching faith in the instructions of your spiritual master. He said to you, “If you ever get money, print books.” And that instruction is now being realized completely in ISKCON. You said that if we just distribute these brilliant books all over the world on a large scale, everything will change for the better. And now we, your grandsons and -daughters, can already see the results. People know and appreciate us more and more, but they don’t know that it is all to your credit. You are not one of those “*gurus*” who promote themselves and their various philosophies to misguide innocent people. You propagate the highest knowledge of the Absolute Truth, which is the only remedy for the condition of all the fallen souls. The most wonderful thing is that you are giving this knowledge freely, and what’s even more, you yourself have undergone severe austerities that nobody else but you could perform.

So how can we repay you for all this? We are trying to do it, at least to some extent, by distributing your books from door to door. In every town and every village we try to give at least one of your books to each person. We also are trying in other ways, such as public programs at least once a week, our *bhajana* and rock groups, *prasādam* distribution, *nāma-haṭṭa,* etc. We would like to increase the intensity of all these programs so that people may learn about Kṛṣṇa and about you, the *ācārya* of this movement. We ask you for all benedictions for this.

I would also like to tell you what people here think about you. When Padayātrā was here in Slovenia, people were always asking about you. One reporter even wanted to have an interview with you. When my brother’s wife visited our temple and saw your *mūrti,* she asked, “Oh, I have seen that man before. He writes these books. How is it that he is in your temple?” There are many similar statements, which prove that even *karmīs* feel your presence.

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you live forever among us, and we humbly beg you to stay with us, because we are simply made of your mercy. And as your grandsons and -daughters, we even have some privilege in this regard. Please confirm this privilege by engaging us even more in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, and give us realizations by which we will be able to stay on the *saṅkīrtana* train going directly to the spiritual world, where we can serve Kṛṣṇa forever.

The servants of your servants in Ljubljana, Slovenia.

## London (city)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

As we approach 1996, the year that marks the 100thAnniversary of your auspicious appearance in this world, all of us, your followers, are considering how we might reveal your glories to the world. Most of the planet’s people are stiff in ignorance of the wonderful opportunities that you labored to make available to them.

But we see that anyone’s life that you do touch becomes powerfully transformed. In spite of your physical absence, your presence is still felt strongly within ISKCON. Because of the depth of your character, thousands of us, even without meeting you personally, have nevertheless managed to develop a deep attachment to you and a commitment to your mission.

No matter what difficulties our movement goes through or whatever shortcomings there are, we can have faith that, when people become aware of you, their faith in Kṛṣṇa consciousness will be born.

Our greatest tribute to you will be when we learn how to actually follow in your footsteps. You made your spiritual master’s mission “one with your heart” and risked all personal inconvenience to fulfill it. This is the example that you have shown us, and many times you drew attention to this formula—“the secret of success in spiritual life”.

“This is the secret of success: After being initiated and receiving the orders of the spiritual master, the disciple should unhesitatingly think about the instructions or orders of the spiritual master and should not allow himself to be disturbed by anything else. This is also the verdict of Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura, who, while explaining a verse of *Bhagavad-gītā* (*vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana,* Bg. 2.41), points out that the order of the spiritual master is the life substance of the disciple. The disciple should not consider whether he is going back home, back to Godhead; his first business should be to execute the order of his spiritual master. Thus a disciple should always meditate on the order of the spiritual master, and that is perfectional meditation. Not only should he meditate upon that order, but he should find out the means by which he can perfectly worship and execute it.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.24.15)

You are waiting to see what we will do with the knowledge and facilities you’ve provided.

Here in London, we, your aspiring servants, feel especially blessed by you. You personally invited Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa here and have allowed us to serve Them. They are so kind to Their servants. And the scope for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in London is unlimited. Thousands of conditioned souls right on our doorstep. We think that we have the nicest little temple in the world. There is a big weight on our shoulders—your expectations. Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara must always receive first-class worship. There must be a daily *hari-nāma* on Oxford Street. The people of this city must find out about Kṛṣṇa consciousness through your books. They must visit Kṛṣṇa’s temple to receive His *darśana* and *prasādam* and never want to leave. From London we must preach to the world.

This last year we have been trying to improve the temple building and our reception of guests. Kṛṣṇa has sent us devotees to help. Our next challenge will be to build that “imposing” temple that you wanted here.

Fulfilling your expectations is no small matter. But we know that we will be empowered to do so by the Lord when we fully dedicate ourselves to your service, giving up all other considerations.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for adventing yourself in this world. Thank you for taking the trouble to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness to us, undeserving as we are. Thank you for your continued patience in spite of our lack of loyalty, our foolishness, our mistakes. Please continue to bless us with your kindness.

Your servants in London (city), U.K.

## London (south)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who single-handedly established this wonderful Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, which is destined to prevail over the earth for the coming ten thousand years.

You have inspired thousands and hundreds of thousands of fallen conditioned souls to join your movement and engage in *bhakti-yoga*. Through your Bhaktivedanta purports, you have kindled a fire in the hearts of these men and women, which will destroy the unlimited material forest of sense desires that reside within their hearts. In order to save the most fallen, you left Vṛndāvana, the land of Kṛṣṇa, at an advanced age, to go to the hell of the Western world. We are very grateful that you were so courageous to leave Vṛndāvana. However, you actually never left Vṛndāvana: you simply transformed wherever you went into Vṛndāvana.

Your potency is so powerful that even after you departed for the spiritual world, you continue to transform places into Vṛndāvana through your disciples and books. Within a short period of time you established temples all over the world, initiated disciples in numerous countries, translated the main *śāstras,* and wrote your transcendental purports. How is it possible for one person to accomplish so much in such a small space of time?

In his *Upadeśāmṛta* Rūpa Gosvāmī perfectly describes you:

*vāco vegaṁ manasaḥ krodha-vegaṁ*  
*jihvā-vegam udaropastha-vegam*  
*etān vegān yo viṣaheta dhīraḥ*  
*sarvām apīmāṁ pṛthivīṁ sa śiṣyāt*

**“A sober person who can tolerate the urge to speak, the mind’s demands, the actions of anger, and the urges of the tongue, belly, and genitals is qualified to make disciples all over the world.”**

Being empowered by the Supreme Lord, you single-handedly conquered the whole world. We are helplessly trying to engage in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Just like the small bird who was trying to dry up the ocean with his beak, we are trying to preach and run one center in South London, but this seems so overwhelming. But single-handedly you conquered the whole world.

Although we are incapable and unqualified, we are hoping to please you by engaging in devotional service under the guidance of your disciples. We, your grandchildren, are begging you to shower your mercy upon us so that we can fully assist your disciples in your Centennial celebrations.

Even to glorify a fraction of your pastimes and personality is impossible. However, having been given this great opportunity on your Vyāsa-pūjā, we are feeling your divine presence in South London.

Eternally grateful to Your Divine Grace,  
Your grandchildren in South London, UK.

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, who have redirected the course of our lives and the lives of millions of other lost *jīvas*.

On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, and on the eve of your Centennial, we try to express in words the depth of your love and mercy upon us, but since that mercy is flowing unlimitedly, we are certainly unable to fully capture the extent of it.

By contact with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our otherwise meaningless lives have become meaningful, just as so many zeros become meaningful when the number one is put before them. You are that number-one Vaiṣṇava who have so mercifully appeared within our lives. Although it appears that you have left us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, still we are able to contact you daily in your instructions, through which you touch us deeply with your divine presence.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura wrote a verse about the influence of a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa in this world, even after his departure:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!

By your life’s example you have proved the truths of this verse. After spending a lifetime in preparation, you left the comfortable atmosphere of Vṛndāvana and came to us in the Western world. With great faith in the words of your spiritual master and in the power of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa, you have spread Kṛṣṇa’s message and His holy names to every town and village, fulfilling the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

When you came to us from Vṛndāvana, you brought that Vṛndāvana atmosphere with you, along with so many other gifts for us. You gave us gifts of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra,* our Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, the association of devotees, temples dedicated to the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, and the perpetual opportunity to serve your mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the *dīkṣā-guru* for some, but the *śikṣā-guru* for us all. So in that sense, since we are all your disciples, your Vyāsa-pūjā—or in other words your worship—is our life and soul. Once a year it affords us the opportunity to openly express our devotional sentiments and appreciation for such a rare Vaiṣṇava as you. On this day, sitting at your lotus feet making our offerings, we feel particularly embarrassed as we see our impurities revealed more clearly in your divine presence. On your Vyāsa-pūjā we decorate the temple with hundreds of flowers, leaves, and branches and we offer you an equal number of opulent preparations, gifts, and garlands. But in our minds we decorate the temple with hundreds of millions of flowers and offer you hundreds of millions of sumptuous Vedic preparations. So may these external arrangements be just an expression of our true inner feelings of gratitude and love for you. We pray that we may celebrate your Vyāsa-pūjā not just ceremoniously once a year, but daily by allowing our lives to be molded by your divine instructions. May we celebrate with resolve to open up a new year of sincere service, surrender, and cooperation and to finish up our business in this material world once and for all, and may we rededicate our lives to your mission of distributing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone.

So on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, may we with one-pointed attention hear about, glorify, remember, and worship you, our beloved spiritual master, the divine representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, and the protector and ever well-wisher of the devotees.

Your insignificant servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopinātha Mandir in Longdenville, Trinidad.

## Madrid

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

*nimajjyonmajjatāṁ ghore bhavābdhau paramāyaṇam*  
*santo brahma-vidaḥ śāntā naur dṛḍhevāpsu majjatām*

**“The devotees of the Lord, peacefully fixed in the absolute knowledge, are the last shelter for those who are constantly going up and down in the horrible ocean of material life. Those devotees are exactly the same as a solid ship which comes to rescue those people who are about to drown” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.26.32).**

This verse makes very clear the magnificence of the pure devotees of the Lord who are preaching actively in the material world, along with the great misery of the conditioned souls who wander completely lost in this *saṁsāra* ocean. There is no way the suffering souls can understand by themselves what their situation is or how to reach the last aim of human life—how to become peaceful, happy, and perfect souls. Only the valuable and beneficial company of a devotee of the Lord, perfect in self-realization and situated beyond the illusory energy, can enlighten the gloomy hearts of the confused souls. This is so because only a pure devotee of the Supreme Lord knows in fullness the Supreme Transcendental Reality, Kṛṣṇa, and the proper means to enlighten the consciousness of the captivated soul. Only the genuinely empowered representative of Kṛṣṇa, through his firm and merciful guidance, can endow the impoverished beings with the inherent and marvelous qualities of the servants of the Lord. In that way, those who were previously discouraged and entangled are now with great confidence and joy progressing toward the highest liberation, love of Godhead.

Thus your glorious accomplishments in this world, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can never be overestimated. In fact, you have saved so many souls and are still saving a lot more. The people who come to know you cannot but feel impressed by your great spiritual power and your extraordinary capacity to attract people to the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You are like a touchstone that can turn into gold the useless base metal of our lives. Before knowing you, we were greatly suffering the heavy lashes of the material nature, and we had no idea about what was going on or why we were insistently being hit with so much violence, nor did we know if there was some way to improve our lives. So, in our ignorance, we were frustrated and desperate almost to the point of becoming crazy. Poisoned with unrestricted sense gratification, we were losing our good sense and covering ourselves more and more with the heavy and dangerous reactions of so much sinful activity.

Thus, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us always recognize clearly the unfortunate position you rescued us from. Then we will always worship you with full conviction and enthusiasm, spreading your teachings and in this way blessing all those who come in contact with the activities of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. The only thing we have to do is deliver to them the transcendental medicines you have given us as gifts: the holy name, *prasādam*, your books, and so on. People who somehow or other receive these gifts are immunized against *māyā,* and they can see the great value of this international association of devotees you founded. If they begin to take full advantage of your gifts, they soon appreciate the countless advantages they can obtain by following your instructions.

We want to take advantage of this occasion to ask you for your blessings, so that we will never stop appreciating your person and activities or deviate from the clear and conclusive path of devotional service you preached. Let our hearts always get pure pleasure from imbibing your spiritual messages in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and your other books, and let us always aim to have a heart like yours.

Please allow us to engage in your ecstatic devotional service forever, in the company of your devotees within your glorious Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

We humbly beg your pardon for the many offenses, weaknesses, hesitations, confusions, and problems we have caused, and we prostrate ourselves in surrender in the shade of your lotus feet.

Your unworthy and unqualified servants in Madrid, Spain.

## Manchester

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Somewhere on some soaring reach,  
in ancient and unmoving trance,  
while charged with all the world to teach,  
the sage divines each ordinance.

Unseen by the unseeing eye,  
he sits, his silent stillness hides  
his heart, which moves to codify  
the love and beauty he confides.

In sacred stanza, deep and pure,  
In each, essential truth revealed.  
By syllable and sentence sure,  
our earthly sentence is repealed.

Yet sometimes seen in midst of men,  
in pastimes on an earthly plane,  
Though seemingly in mortal span,  
eternity is his domain.

And seen again in every lord  
and master of unending line,  
Conveying every timeless word,  
translated timely to the time.

Today we praise his glories here,  
in you redounding and replete.  
The words and wisdom of the seer  
delivered unto us complete.

Dear Prabhupāda, please hear our prayer  
in supplication for your care:  
we beg from you the strength to share  
the burden of the love you bear.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. Thank you for allowing us to remain another year engaged in the service of your mission. We find it difficult to adequately praise you. Perhaps the truest testimony of your glory is seen in your many followers who remain fixed in your orders. Surely in our Vaiṣṇava history it is hardly seen that a preacher leaves behind him so many others who powerfully continue his mission. As ISKCON continues to flourish and solidify, so your glory becomes more and more manifest. The world is amazed, the pious being filled with joy and the detractors consumed by envy. Thus everyone is caught in the spreading network of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s movement, enraptured by its power and majesty.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you sometimes said that you were synonymous with your society, ISKCON. Thus to us you are like a great tree whose roots are Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and the *Vedas,* whose trunk is the eternal Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya,* whose branches are your sincere followers, and whose fruits and flowers are nectarean love of God. May that wonderful tree continue to grow and provide shelter for all living entities who are suffering the scorching sun of the Kali-yuga.

Your humble servants in Manchester, UK.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-dharma dāsa)

## Manipur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

As the Centennial celebration of your divine appearance fast approaches, the devotees from around the world are working hard to bring to fruition the giant projects undertaken to offer at your lotus feet. Our humble attempt to construct Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Maṇimandir, the divine home of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandrajī, needs your special mercy, since Manipur, which was once a Vaiṣṇava land, is now getting the worst of the Age of Kali. The original Vaiṣṇava spirit of purity, simplicity, and humility is being replaced by contamination, crookedness, and false pride. Due to sinful activities, people are suffering from the threefold miseries of material existence. But unfortunately they have no eyes to see that they are suffering because they have deliberately neglected to render pure devotional service to the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are the most fallen, unqualified children in your family. We have no resources or good intelligence, and we are unsurrendered also. We need your blessings the most. Kindly shower your causeless mercy upon us so that we can revive the original spirit of Vaiṣṇavism in this land by preaching the glories of the holy name and establishing the eternal home of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandrajī.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your children at ISKCON Manipur, India.

## Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your beautiful lotus feet. Thank you for allowing us to have your *darśana* at yet another location here in Māyāpur—a most prominent one—your Puṣpa-samādhi Mandira.

In your bamboo and straw *bhajana-kuṭira* near the main gate—your first residence in Māyāpur when there were only rice fields—you give the living example of your motto for spiritual life: Simple living and high thinking. Where your disciples could see only open fields and little else, you saw where a great spiritual city would rise, manifesting the vision of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura. We bow down to you in your humble residence and pray for your blessings: May we imbibe your ways of perfect simplicity and pure thought, eagerly working to implement the glorious vision of the previous *ācāryas*.

Between two wide verandahs on the second floor of the Lotus Building, as “Vaikuṇṭha breezes” blow, you sit on a white *āsana* in your room behind your desk, illuminating the statements of *śāstra* with your Bhaktivedanta Purports.
