It is here that you nourish disciples and guests with nectar from your lips and make transcendentally revolutionary plans for the world, beginning with plans for this holiest of *dhāmas*. Let us prostrate ourselves on the white-sheeted mat before you and thank you for being so accessible to us in your conversations, lectures, letters, and books. May we accept your words—both the soft encouraging words and the hard correcting words—as our very life and soul, again and again and again.

At the back of the temple room you sit on your *vyāsāsana*, gazing intently at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, who smilingly gaze back at you and your followers. “There is no question of separation” between you and Kṛṣṇa, even as you seem to be some thirty meters away from the altar. In your presence, as you expand the eternal truths of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in your sublime, straightforward lectures, it becomes clear that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You cut through all possible misconceptions, making bright and clear the real path of perfection. Let us offer our worship to you daily in *guru-pūjā,* with *ārati,* flowers, *kīrtana,* and heartfelt *praṇāmas,* remembering always how you open our darkened eyes to the dazzling splendor of spiritual existence, knowledge, and bliss. May we be blessed by you to properly see and serve Their Lordships Śrī Nṛsiṁha-deva, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava and the Sakhīs.

And now also in your imposing yet graceful Puṣpa-samādhi Mandira, you sit regally in a vaulted chamber of marble and gold, bigger than life, with kind but determined countenance, tirelessly receiving the throngs coming for your *darśana* and blessings. Although it is not a place you sat during your manifest pastimes, we perceive your active presence here as commander of a great world mission as you sit facing your own commanders, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura at his *samādhi* and Lord Caitanya at His birthplace to the north. It seems to be here especially that you supervise the grand projects of your movement, reminding us that “Impossible is a word in a fool’s dictionary” and that there must be “work now, *samādhi* later.” Here you are indeed present in *samādhi*—full absorption in the transcendental activities of Lord Caitanya in His mood of the perfect devotee of the Lord. Let us prostrate ourselves before you here, offering flowers of surrender to you in your Puṣpa-samādhi, and then let us stand with folded hands awaiting your orders. May we always hear your directions clearly and carry them out with simplicity and determination, with no motivation except to please you.

In the awesome atmosphere of your Puṣpa-samādhi we are humbled. You are indeed drawing the whole world’s attention to your lotus feet, and we, your servants, find ourselves in the limelight of the glowing effulgence of your transcendental fame and glory. Please let us, shedding all pettiness of selfishness and fear, live up to the legacy of your greatness. Please allow us to “do the needful” to make of the Māyāpur Project a truly spiritual city from which the whole world will take inspiration, and to where the whole world can come for an eternal festival of *nāma-saṅkīrtana*. You write in a *Bhāgavatam* purport (5.19.24):

“The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement has established its center in Māyāpur, the birthsite of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, to give men the great opportunity to go there and perform a constant festival of *saṅkīrtana-yajña,* as recommended herein (*yajñeśa-makhā mahotsavāḥ*) and to distribute *prasādam* to millions of hungry people hankering for spiritual emancipation. This is the mission of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.”

From your chamber here in your Puṣpa-samādhi you are exhibiting the factual activities of *samādhi,* conducting the eternal *saṅkīrtana* of Lord Caitanya. Let everyone come and receive your blessings here in your Puṣpa-samādhi Mandira, and then let us humbly help you engage all in the constant festival of *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Offering you heaps of flowers of surrender, we beg you again and again to please let us steadily become more qualified to serve and assist you in your super-excellent service to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava.

Begging to be your humble servants, the residents of the Māyāpur Project, West Bengal, India.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṣetra dāsa)

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

We are presenting to Your Divine Grace this Vyāsa-pūjā offering, praying to Lord Kṛṣṇa to give us the understanding that you are the savior of the world and the purest devotee of the Supreme Lord, who is powerfully pushing forward the mission of guiding humanity for the next ten thousand years.

The success of your preaching work is inconceivable. As Lord Ananta Śeṣa with His thousands of mouths eternally sings the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa but never finishes because they are unlimited, so also thousands of devotees and surrendered Vaiṣṇavas constantly sing your glories but never finish because your glories are also unlimited.

We feel so fortunate and also unworthy of being under your shelter and guidance.

Your transcendental name is and will be spread all over the three worlds because of your infinite mercy and complete purity and obedience to the orders of your divine spiritual master.

With the arrival of the most auspicious Centennial celebration, all of the devotees feel so glad—even the most pessimistic become optimistic, and the most fallen get up and wish to stay away from *māyā*. We feel so indebted to you that we wish to work together just to please you.

By your great power, all of us feel inspired to serve you by preaching your glories and serving your mission.

By your mercy we feel that you are always with us, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Wishing to eternally serve you and hoping to always have your *darśana,* humbly falling in the shade of your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we remain

Your insignificant servants in Mexico City, Mexico.

## Miami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

When you visited Miami in 1975, you remarked that the palm trees and tropical vegetation reminded you of India. In the evenings you gave *Bhagavad-gītā* lectures outside, sitting beneath the huge banyan trees at the Coconut Grove temple. The temple is still in Coconut Grove, but no longer in the rented house you visited. Now, by your blessings, we have a beautiful former Christian church with attached buildings and restaurant.

You told us that the success of everything depends on how Kṛṣṇa is satisfied: “So if you try to satisfy Kṛṣṇa, then whatever you want, He will give you, benedict.” We have been trying, in our humble way, to please both you and Lord Kṛṣṇa by book distribution for over twenty years now. Devotees are still going daily to the airport to distribute books, and that is basically the only way this temple has ever been maintained. We see it as a benediction from you and Lord Kṛṣṇa that we have such a wonderful preaching facility.

In Jakarta in 1973 someone asked you, “When did this movement start?” You told them, “This movement started five thousand years ago when Kṛṣṇa appeared. We have not manufactured this movement; we are just pushing on the same movement.”

Here in Miami we are blessed with sunny weather all year around, and the devotees go on *hari-nāma* six days of the week to South Miami Beach, downtown Miami, or Coconut Grove.

You taught us that a simple life centered on devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa is actually a sign of spiritual opulence. In the modern world, material amenities and luxurious living are taken to be the goal, but they entangle one deeper in illusion and suffering. “We are not for material opulence—we are for ideal character. . . . First of all you have to learn how to become humble. Here all the people are educated how to become proud.”

You also taught us how to achieve full, universal happiness (*viśvaṁ pūrṇa-sukhāyate*): “We can make them all happy by Kṛṣṇa consciousness, if they follow our instructions, even in this material world.” We are very happy because we have just installed, for your pleasure and the pleasure of the Deities, the most extraordinarily gorgeous marble *vyāsāsana* and *siṁhāsana,* both hand-carved in Jaipur.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you struggled so hard to make us Kṛṣṇa conscious: “They have forgotten Kṛṣṇa and I am trying to make them Kṛṣṇa conscious. It is a very difficult job. I have to shed my blood three tons before I make one convinced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” If we can only remember this, then we can execute our own daily service with greater enthusiasm.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we agree with what His Holiness Bhaktivikāśa Swami wrote in his book *Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda:* “It is doubtful if any spiritual master since Lord Caitanya has evoked so much affection from so many people. . . . Śrīla Prabhupāda is the most important personality in centuries.”

We sincerely pray that we may be able to serve you and glorify you forever. Please keep us in the shade of your lotus feet.

Your eternal servants in Miami, Florida.

## Monterey

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus like feet.

On this must auspicious day we want to worship you by remembering your divine instructions to us all. Please accept this humble offering. Every morning we offer this prayer to Your Divine Grace:

*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya,*  
*ār nā koriho mane āśā*  
*śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei se uttama-gati,*  
*je prasāde pūre sarva āśā*

**“Make the teachings from the lotus mouth of the spiritual master one with your heart, and do not desire anything else. Attachment to the lotus feet of the spiritual master is the best means of spiritual advancement. By his mercy all desires for spiritual perfection are fulfilled” (“Śrī Guru-vandanā,” from Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura’s** ***Prema-bhakti-candrikā*****).**

Please let us fully understand the meaning of these lines so that we may be able to live with you eternally in union by service in separation.

**To live with you in union by service in separation**

In your purports and translations you have given us your eternal guidelines and your divine, eternal association. These serve as a divine source of inspiration enabling us to live a life of full dedication, united with you by service in separation.

**You live with us forever**

“To Śrīla Prabhupāda Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, MY SPIRITUAL MASTER … He lives forever by his divine instructions and the follower lives with him.” On that very first page of your masterwork, the divine translation of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* you have taught us how to live united with you by service in separation.

**We are never separated**

“The disciple and spiritual master are never separated because the spiritual master always keeps company with the disciple as long as the disciple follows strictly the instructions of the spiritual master” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.28.47).

You wrote this as our guideline, as a source of inspiration for us to be united with you by service in separation. To keep fresh our divine connection wit you, we have to push forward your divine mission and follow your instructions in a humble mood of co-operation; that will keep us united with you by service in separation.

**Your warning to us**

“Unfortunately, when the *ācārya* disappears, rogues and nondevotees take advantage and immediately begin to introduce unauthorized principles in the name of so-called *svāmīs, yogīs,* philanthropists and welfare workers” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.28.48).

Please give us clear intelligence so that we may stay united with you by service in separation.

**We should not become bookworms**

“If one becomes a bookworm, reading many books and scriptures and hearing many commentaries and instructions of many men, this will produce doubt within his heart. One cannot in this way ascertain the real goal of life” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi* 16.11).

Please give us strength so that our attention may not be diverted in any way, so that we may remain united with you by service in separation.

“One must give up the ambition to be a learned scholar and in this way earn worldly reputation and financial facilities. If one diverts his attention to studying many books, he cannot fix his mind in devotional service, nor can he understand many scriptures, for they are full of grave statements and meanings” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi* 16.11, purport).

You have made things very clear for us in the above purport and translation. Please give us the power to give up the desire for any kind of worldly aspiration, so that we may ever keep fresh our union with you by service in separation.

**If one very carefully reads**

“Nor should one be enthusiastic about reading various types of books, save and except the ones which lead us to the advancement of devotional service. Practically, if one very carefully reads *Bhagavad-gītā*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *Teachings of Lord Caitanya* and this *Nectar of Devotion*, that will give him sufficient knowledge to understand the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. One need not take the trouble of reading other books” (*Nectar of Devotion*, Ch. 7).

Give us the required peace of mind to be able to have the care and concentration to study your purports and translations. Please let us have simple intelligence to follow these instructions and to understand our union with you by service in separation.

**In the devotees’ association**

“When we are speaking, hearing and chanting, it means that one not only should chant and hear the holy name of the Lord as Rāma, Kṛṣṇa (or systematically Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare), but one should also read and hear the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in the association of devotees” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 2.2.30, purport).

Please give us the strength to give up any excuse for not pushing on your divine mission in union with you by service in separation.

**These four works are sufficient**

“In our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement we have therefore limited our study of the Vedic literatures to the *Bhagavad-gītā*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*. These four works are sufficient for preaching purposes. They are adequate for the understanding of the philosophy and the spreading of missionary activities all over the world. That is the principle. By thoroughly studying a limited number of books, one can understand the philosophy” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 22.118).

You have given us very clear instructions: which books to read, the goal, and the principle. Please give us the intelligence to understand them properly so that we will be able to remain with you, united by service in separation.

**Without reading, bhakti-yoga is simply a waste of time**

“The spiritual master prescribes standard literature for the prosecution of yoga of devotional service, and he himself speaks only from scriptural reference. Therefore reading standard scriptures is necessary for executing *yoga*. Practicing *yoga* without reading the standard literature is simply a waste of time” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.28.4, purport).

Please let us be absorbed in reading and distributing your divine books; in this way we can keep ourselves united with you by service in separation.

**Otherwise, after some time they will fall down**

“Men become strong and stout by eating sufficient grains, but the devotee who simply eats ordinary grains but does not taste the transcendental pastimes of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Kṛṣṇa gradually becomes weak and falls down from the transcendental position. However, if one drinks but a drop of the nectar of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes, his body and mind begin to bloom. and he begins to laugh, sing and dance. \[Purport:\] All the devotees connected with this Kṛṣṇa Consciousness movement must read all the books that have been translated (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *Bhagavad-gītā*, and others); otherwise, after some time, they will simply eat, sleep and fall down from their position. Thus they will miss the opportunity to attain an eternal, blissful life of transcendental pleasure” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 25.278).

Please let us fully understand all these instructions, for we want to center our life on your divine service to be able to tolerate your separation.

Your servants in Monterey, Mexico.

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, but my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*śrī-caitanya-mano-’bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your effulgent *kṛṣṇa-śakti* has opened all our eyes to the real purpose of our formerly senseless lives. Although born in darkness, we are allowed, by your mercy, to newly bathe in your inexhaustible purity. Your potent *kīrtana,* saturated with the ś*uddha-sattva* of your *prema-bhakti,* has worked a miracle in our lives. Penetrating deep into the tenebrous caverns of our mixed hearts, your purity awakened us from the coma of illusion, giving us all our spiritual rebirth and propelling us into the eternal dance of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana-līlā*. Now our lives are a true celebration as we endeavor to become purified and truly worthy of your shelter.

To become your real followers is now our lives’ real sustenance. It’s not so cheap and not so easily attained. Crippled by a chronic unwillingness to be truly *dāsānudāsa*—servants of your servants—we drag along our anchor-*anarthas* of self-importance and foolish pride, wallowing too often in the muck of shallow enviousness and petty politics, defeating our own progress and hindering the spirit of love and trust that is your modus operandi. Your shining example of universal compassion allows us to rise above our pervasive inadequacies as we endeavor to imbibe your mood of selfless sacrifice to help suffering mankind so that all *jīvas* can attain the shelter of the *mahā-vadānya-avatāra,* the most munificent incarnation, Śrī Caitanya-deva. This is your transforming legacy, sustaining our individual and collective purposes and giving real meaning to our lives.

As we eagerly approach the glorious Centennial celebration of your auspicious appearance, a hallmark in the history of mankind, we find a renewed inspiration to become more united in the loving spirit you constantly showed to us all. We thus hanker to bathe our weary hearts and minds in the ever-fresh nectar of your expanding glories. Attempting with imperfect means to make your movement the great spiritual force it is destined to be in the re-direction of wayward humankind, we pray for the precious realization of your continuing guidance in our hearts. Your living presence in all our lives is the one great hope nourishing us toward the attainment of your eternal association.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your unworthy servants in ISKCON Montreal, Canada.

## Murāri Sevaka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for traveling the world over and preaching the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are so indebted to you for lifting us up from the mire of forgetfulness that we owe you our total existence. We have found that the goal of life is to re-establish our intrinsic, eternal bond of friendship with and service to Kṛṣṇa. This most intimate bond of affection has been lost since time immemorial. The only way to revive that relationship is through the spiritual master.

Our wonderful system of disciplic succession, coming down from Kṛṣṇa Himself, helps the individual souls remember Him. On our own we would never have stumbled upon the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness taught by this disciplic succession, for we were born into dark ignorance. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the light that extinguishes this darkness. Your excellent devotional service—all that you do, from translating the Vedic scriptures to your practical example in life for us to follow—inspires all of us to see and appreciate the greatness of Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master.

May our appreciation of you grow eternally, and may we always stay in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

I remember how His Holiness Jayapatāka Mahārāja once expressed his appreciation for you in a lecture. His booming voice filling the air with adamant desperation, he exclaimed, “What would we have done if Śrīla Prabhupāda had not come to America?! What would our position be?!!” The answer is that we would all be lost without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

All the devotees here at Murāri Sevaka are celebrating your appearance day with love and enthusiasm, remembering you and all that you’ve done to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. Please bestow your kindness upon us all so that we may excel in glorifying you and Kṛṣṇa, for without the mercy of the spiritual master we can’t make any advancement.

Yesterday while offering *ārati* to the members of the disciplic succession, I began meditating on each *ācārya* by seeing in my mind’s eye the accomplishments, pastimes, and qualities of each personality down the line. When I got to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I remembered your coming to America by ship and landing, your early New York pastimes, including chanting in the park and establishing ISKCON, your traveling around the world and being welcomed by your disciples at your various temples, your training them, your showing deep compassion for them even when they failed to come up to the standard, your installing Deities and dancing in festivals—in short, your *saṅkīrtana-līlā*.

Because you are an intimate servant of Lord Caitanya, your pastimes and qualities are unlimited, like those of the Lord Himself.

Often I’ve thought, “Next time around I would like to be at your Second Avenue storefront temple, under your personal association, guidance, and loving friendship.”

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at the Murāri Sevaka farm in Tennessee.  
(written by Nandanācārya dāsa and Nirguṇa dāsa)

## Murwillumbah

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Today we remember your holy birth,  
The day you so kindly first blessed this earth.  
At that time none could foresee your worth,  
Save an astrologer who foretold all.

Though you quietly hid from us your true glory,  
In hindsight now the truth is forthcoming.  
We’re unraveling, through *śāstra,* the real story  
Of the great emissary sent from the Lord.

The prediction of you is there in print:  
“Along will come my *sena-pati bhakta*.”  
Lord Gaurāṅga spoke this, and here is the hint:  
“He’ll preach to the people outside of India.”

Locan dās quotes this clear as a bell.  
“Leader of the army”—it can only be you!  
Earlier still are suggestions as well;  
The *Padma Purāṇa* contains such a clue.

“Holding the mace,” you said of your teacher.  
How much more shall we say of you?  
You are Kali-yuga’s greatest preacher;  
The compassion you showed is unrivaled.

You are glorious, and your fame will spread.  
By your potency your mission will succeed.  
Oh, *ācārya* for the next ten thousand years!  
Allow us to assist you in some small way.

By our serving you, all goals will be attained.  
First *vāsudeva-kathā-ruci* will be ours,  
Then freedom from lust to enjoy will be gained,  
And at last the knot in the heart will be pierced.

Not just for selfish gain we wish to please.  
But your lotus feet offer soothing solace,  
And you captured our hearts by your qualities—  
Your dedication, love, and compassion.

O holy master! This one prayer we repeat:  
Forgive our faults and shower us with mercy.  
We beg this as we cling to your feet:  
Forgive our faults and shower us with mercy.

From your aspiring servants at the New Govardhana farm in Murwillumbah, Australia.  
(written by Ambikā-devī dāsī)

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

You are definitely very dear to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He did not personally go out of India to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness but chose to send you to the Western countries to spread His message. You succeeded in establishing Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world. It is by your mercy and endeavor that Lord Caitanya’s followers can be seen all over the surface of the earth. Following in your footsteps, these devotees are not only practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but they have also developed a strong mood of spreading Lord Caitanya’s sublime mission in every corner of the world. They are trying their best to respiritualize the planet once again. All the credit goes to you for what has been accomplished already and what will be accomplished in the future. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, gave the direction and vision of how to glorify the Supreme Lord in all circumstances. You showed the true meaning of *yukta-vairāgya* and of unalloyed devotional service to the Lord.

We have heard about your desire to have an ISKCON center in a city located in central India, and Nagpur is the most important city in this part of the country. It used to be part of the kingdom of Vidarbha, which was ruled by the famous and pious Mahārāja Bhīṣmaka, the father of Rukmiṇī. Lord Kṛṣṇa personally came here to subdue the demoniac kings who had come to assist Śiśupāla in seeking Rukmiṇī’s hand in marriage. This land was also blessed by Lord Rāma when He was on His way to Daṇḍakāraṇya. By your mercy, we have an ISKCON center here dedicated to preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the kingdom of Vidarbha.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg at your lotus feet on this auspicious day of your appearance to please bestow your mercy upon us so that we may prove ourselves worthy grandsons of Your Divine Grace.

Your servants of ISKCON Nagpur, India.

## Nandagram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you and your followers!

We are always trying to be happy in this material world. But it is just like trying to be happy in a prison. Luckily we did not learn this too late, and it is never too late to mend. By the mercy of our Guru Mahārāja, your disciple, we have gradually taken to Kṛṣṇa consciousness and are getting to know that the highest goal of human life is to serve the Lord and attain pure love for Him. All this comes directly from your mercy. Now we even have the chance to worship the Deities, though we are not yet qualified. Next year is the Centennial of your appearance. We sincerely hope that you would kindly give us more of your blessings, and by that time we will become eligible servants of your servants and make more and more devotees.

Hare Krishna!

The servants of your servants in Nandagram, Khasadeśa.

## Newcastle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances,

Just as you thanked Nārada Muni for kindly agreeing to appear in a particular chapter of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* the devotees at your Newcastle temple would like to thank you for kindly agreeing to appear in your *mūrti* in the temple room. In one of your first filmed appearances, you gravely stated that your mission was to propagate the teachings of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Looking at your *mūrti* form reminds us of your whole lifetime of enthusiasm for the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Looking at your *mūrti* form, we try to find the inspiration to seriously carry on your mission. On the altar we worship the Pañca-tattva under your supervision, and we dance before Their Lordships for your pleasure. In Kali-yuga, persons who are sufficiently endowed with your mercy worship Lord Caitanya. Only by devotion to you is it possible to understand the glories of Lord Gaurāṅga and enter into the fire of the *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us appreciate more the transcendental nature of your activities, and always keep us engaged in your service.

Your servants in Newcastle, the United Kingdom.

## New Dvārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The sound vibration of your voice is the most soothing tone to be found here in this material world. When you sing the songs of the Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas* or lead the devotees in the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* all fear, anxiety, and doubt fly away from the heart. It is inconceivable to your unremitting disciples and servants to want to hear any other reverberation. Modern technology is providing access to your transcendental sound vibration. Though it is now almost eighteen years after your departure, still I can hear your separated energy in the form of lectures and *bhajan*s. Remarkable it is that, although I listen to your tapes over and over again, every day they appear fresh and vibrant. The vibration of your voice is always prominent in the ears of your disciples. Naturally, they cannot hear anything else. Constant remembrance of the holy sound of your voice keeps them enlightened and enlivened, and they do not allow any other sound to enter their ears. Since their attention is fixed on your voice, they cannot divert their minds to any other subject. In other words, a disciple who has heard the sound of your voice forgets to talk or hear of any other subject. A serious disciple of yours who hears this transcendental vibration becomes so accustomed to it that he cannot divert his attention to any subject matter not related to your blissful characteristics and paraphernalia.

In the Second Chapter of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* Lord Kṛṣṇa points out to Arjuna that when one has passed out of the dense forest of delusion, one becomes indifferent to all that has been heard and all that is to be heard. Your ever-faithful followers know this very well. Nothing compares to your Bhaktivedanta purports, for without them, reading the Vedic literatures would be an impossible chore. You have already given more than everything in your Bhaktivedanta purports, and indeed, one who thinks he can digest the complete feast of your writings in one lifetime is surely fooling himself to the highest degree, what to speak of actually realizing them. Some of us cannot help but understand the scriptures only through you. Some of us take you as our eternal spiritual master, and we long for that day when we will hear you personally speak on Kṛṣṇa again.

Some of us can’t help but see you as our eternal well-wisher. For those of us who still regard you in the same chaste and loyal manner as we did during your manifest presence, nothing compares to you. We were always looking for the perfect example, the ideal, the *ācārya,* and when we became causelessly fortunate to come in contact with you, we were satisfied beyond our imaginative puissance. Some of us, even after so many years, have doubts regarding what you expect from your disciples. Perhaps if you were to manifest just one more time at the Māyāpur Festival in 1996 for your Centennial celebration, then once and for all the myriad questions and controversies that have plagued ISKCON since November 14, 1977, could be resolved.

The prayers of Prahlāda Mahārāja to Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva in glorification of his spiritual master, Nārada Muni, quite fittingly depict the position of Your Divine Grace and one who wants to be your eternal follower. (We reproduce them here from your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* 7.9.28, 43, 44, with slight editing changes.)

“My dear Lord, because of our association with material desires, one after another, we were gradually falling into a blind well full of snakes, following the general populace. But your servant, Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly accepted us as his disciples and instructed us how to achieve the transcendental position. Therefore, our first duty is to serve him. How could we ever leave his service?”

“O best of the great personalities, Śrīla Prabhupāda is not at all afraid of material existence, for wherever he stays he is fully absorbed in thoughts of Your glories and activities. His concern is only for the fools and rascals who are making elaborate plans for material happiness and maintaining their families, societies and countries. He is simply concerned with love for them.”

“We see that there are many so-called saintly persons indeed, but they are interested only in their own deliverance or so-called spiritual elevation. Not caring for the big cities and towns, they go to the Himalayas or the forest to meditate with vows of silence. They are not interested in delivering others. As for Śrīla Prabhupāda, however, he did not wish to be liberated alone, leaving aside all of us poor fools and rascals. He knew that without Kṛṣṇa consciousness, without taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, one cannot be happy. Therefore, he wished to bring them back to the shelter of Your Lordship’s lotus feet.”

The mere fact that I have been asked to write this offering is confirmation that you are everyone’s well-wisher eternally. Because you executed the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta, the disciplic succession remained unbroken. Indeed, if you had not executed your spiritual master’s order, where would any of us be now?

Your servants in New Dvārakā, Los Angeles, California.  
(written by Nirantara dāsa)

## New Goloka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to the Vaiṣṇava devotees of the Lord.

*vāñchā-kalpatarubhyaś ca kṛpā-sindhubhya eva ca*  
*patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ*

On this most auspicious celebration day of your appearance, we would like to rededicate ourselves to your transcendental movement. Here at New Goloka Dhāma we are desiring in some small way to assist you and your servants in carrying on your mission. We realize that achieving this goal is possible only if we stick very tightly to all your guidelines and instructions for pure devotional service. Without your mercy and guidance, our lives would have no meaning or direction.

You brought with you the ultimate solution to all of life’s problems—Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray that we may give up all our petty differences and work together to purify not only ourselves but everyone we meet. Please take us in your *saṅkīrtana* party! You go on speaking in your books, and everyday we can see how the hearts of the conditioned souls are being changed by your association. Although we are serving you in separation, we can see how you are continuing to offer shelter to the whole world. The members of the Indian community are always amazed to see how in such a remote place as North Carolina, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are being worshipped as opulently as Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Golokānanda are. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are just like a touchstone, and all those who seriously follow in your footsteps will receive the greatest benediction—pure, unmotivated love for Kṛṣṇa. Thank you for giving us this ISKCON movement, and please engage us always in your service.

Your servants at New Goloka Dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina.  
(written by Kṛṣṇapriyā-devī dāsī)

## New Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. As the very special and auspicious day of the one hundredth anniversary of your appearance on this planet approaches, we can see more and more how significant and exceptional that event is. Having a pure devotee next to us, either by *vapu* or by *vāṇī,* is neither an ordinary nor easy thing. It is extraordinary because, as you have taught us, in Kali-yuga to contact a bona fide spiritual master, an *ācārya,* is like receiving a life buoy while drowning in the middle of the material ocean. And it is not an easy thing to maintain our contact with the *ācārya* because, due to our immature stage of spiritual consciousness, in due course of time we may forget our duty and deviate from your instructions on the path you have mercifully revealed to us.

Once when a devotee confessed his difficulties in continuing the preaching mission, you answered, “Just push on.” When you started your mission, it was also very difficult, but you showed how determination and full faith in following the *guru’s* order are the keys to success.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto’ pi*

Only by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we remain together on the boat of Kṛṣṇa consciousness; only by your mercy will we weather the storm of material life and victoriously cross the ocean of birth and death, which is filled with the high waves of the illusory material energy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you give us everything to win this battle: your boat, ISKCON; Lord Caitanya’s dearmost weapon, the *mahā-mantra;* and favorable winds in the form of your words and those of the *śāstra*. Please give us the strength to take shelter more and more of these three jewels and in that way to rise to the stage of true Prabhupādānugas.

Your servants in New Māyāpura, France.

## New Navadvīpa

*kula-kīrtir iha yathā yadunām*  
*aparā-tata bhagavaj-janmanā*  
*tathaiva taḍīya-sampradāyasya*  
*khyātis tvayā bhāgavatena guro*

**“O my master, the fame of the Yadu family has expanded without limit due to the Lord’s appearance among them. Similarly, the glory of his disciplic succession has spread, and also of His instructions, all because of you, His pure devotee.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Our humblest obeisances unto you again and again. Truly your mercy is all that we are made of. Only by Your Divine Grace’s invitation do Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva agree to reside here and accept our insignificant service. With each passing year, we find ourselves more and more dependent upon you; our eternal debt grows greater and greater.

When your instructions are embraced as the conclusive truth, all philosophical differences are automatically reconciled. We offer all respect to other Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas,* but we are totally sold out to you, for you alone have rescued us from material existence and given us what no one has ever given the whole world before—the opportunity to serve Kṛṣṇa. Therefore our motto is:

Know Prabhupāda—Know Kṛṣṇa  
No Prabhupāda—No Kṛṣṇa

Without you we have no shelter. It is said that a fool and his money are soon parted. Without you we soon discover that we are simply fools with no more money. As the person *Bhāgavatam,* you exemplified the instructions of the book *Bhāgavatam*. Kṛṣṇa says, “Just as fire is covered by smoke, similarly every endeavor is covered by some fault.” Whereas some prefer to focus on the smoke, you operate on the axiomatic truth “Where there is smoke there is fire” and prefer to search out the fire instead. Because your chastisements are intended to guide us back home, back to Godhead, we embrace them as building blocks of our success. We all hanker more and more for that ever-fresh enthusiasm you give us in our every endeavor. Your acknowledgment is the ultimate confirmation of our service, and your encouragement is the life of our movement. You yourself are its very soul.

Just as we are a small island in the Pacific Ocean, similarly, by your mercy, New Navadvīpa is a small island in the ocean of nescience. You are our only hope when the hurricanes of *māyā* regularly sweep these oceans of nescience, and we have complete confidence that as captain of our ship you will safely see us to our home port.

We humbly beg that you please count us among your servants and give us devotional service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, at whose lotus feet you are always to be found.

Hare Krishna!

The devotees of New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii.

## New Tālavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble and respectful obeisances. All glories to you! O faithful servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, you are strongly preaching the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and thus you are delivering the world from the culprits of voidism and impersonalism.

In our 1994 Vyāsa-pūjā offering we prayed for your guidance. As we look back upon the year, we can perceive your inspiration in the form of your books, your devotees, Paramātmā, and the holy name. Certainly you lit our pathway.

This year also promises to be a wonderful year full of expanding devotional service. As your project continues to unfold, we fear an arrest by our unfortunate attraction to material nature. Therefore, we are begging you to please protect us, just as Droṇa and Bhīṣma protected King Duryodhana during the Kurukṣetra War; or as Arjuna protected Sātyakī in the same war, while Bhūriśravā was preparing to kill him. Without your protection, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we fear a relapse of misconduct caused by the attraction of *māyā*. Certainly this can be averted by remaining close to your lotus feet and sincerely following your instructions.

Please beseech the Holy Name to give us a glimpse of the taste of His *darśana*. Please help us to swim in the nectar of the *mahā-mantra,* the Lord’s *prasādam,* His divine pastimes and qualities, His devotees, and His ever-expanding devotional service.

May we always have the opportunity to preach your glories and message. May we always remember and never forget our eternal constitutional position as an atom in the dust of the Supreme Personality of Godhead’s lotus feet. May we never offend your disciples, their disciples, your followers, or anyone in general. Please help us to remain pleasant and undisturbed, always fixed in the reality of the relationship between the *jīvātmā* and Paramātmā.

By your divine mercy, may we awaken to our eternal identity as the servants of the servants of the Lord, many times removed. Thank you for sticking by us. May we continuously try to satisfy you. May these not be useless words but sentences of truth that come to life. Just like you, may we serve our Gurudeva with faith and devotion. This is the key to our spiritual success.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need you more and more. Please forgive our desperation. After one year of not writing you, we feel compelled to appeal for your assistance. Surely we thought of you, prayed to you, glorified you, worshiped you, and served you during 1994. This Vyāsa-pūjā offering is another form of personal service. The Māyāvādīs and mundane persons can never understand the feeling and love of the devotees for the Lord and His bona fide representative, the spiritual master.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please inspire us and allow us to go on serving you more and more.

*Oṁ Tat Sat*

Your servants at the New Tālavana Farm, Mississippi.

## New Vraja (Hungary)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of Your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious occasion, Your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Today it is Thursday, and I am staying at the Budapest temple as a guest. Sitting in the corner of one of the office rooms on the first floor, I become aware of what is going on all around me.

Next door there is a meeting. The Food for Life devotees have just done a massive, six-ton distribution of potatoes in Budapest to the poor and needy. It went down really well—so much favorable media coverage. They are making plans to distribute big quantities of food in the future. Yesterday they were in the city looking at a site where they could set up a kitchen area to run the project from, and where they could also open a small restaurant for the students that attend the college next door.

The phone rings. It’s a friend of mine from the farm we have in southern Hungary, New Vraja Dhāma. He is in charge of building the temple there. My mind goes to the farm—50 surrendered devotees, a 1,000-square-meter temple under construction, 170 acres of land to farm, a *gośālā,* a *gurukula,* and *āśramas* to build. We have a real task ahead, easily enough just in the production and development stage for the next who-knows-how-many years!

The office clears. Just one guest remains; he lives outside and works on a computer, helping produce a magazine that will be distributed by the *saṅkīrtana* devotees on the street. Here we have printed up to the Seventh Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, with translations ready up to the Tenth Canto, and we have also printed the *Ādi-līlā* of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. Last week a devotee distributed 150 *mahā*-big books in one day, collecting around $2,500!

The Nāma-haṭṭa leader walks in. He looks after three Nāma-haṭṭas in the eastern part of the country, and two other devotees look after the rest of the country. The give guidance to hundreds; many of the initiated devotees in the temples have come from the Nāma-haṭṭa groups. He is looking for his friend who coordinates donations to help with the construction on the farm. He gets companies to help by donating bricks, tiles, cement, etc. Due to his sincere service, practically all the large amounts of building materials we need are donated or bought very cheaply.

The temple begins to feel busy. The evening program will start shortly, and guests are starting to come. His Holiness Śivarāma Mahārāja arrived just last night, and all the devotees want to hear his evening class. Tomorrow we will all go to the farm. Mahārāja has brought Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, who will be installed and worshiped there.

All this is going on day by day, yet you did not visit here even once! By the purity of your words and the words of those who faithfully follow you, we all have the opportunity to live this life of sublime ecstasy, performing our daily activities while coming closer and closer to the Lord. All of us from all different backgrounds—small and tall, fat and thin, poor and rich, intelligent and bewildered—owe our spiritual lives to you, who have this amazing ability to bring us all together and give us a goal and purpose in life, engaging our skills and energies in the service of the Supreme Lord. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Somehow or other we are all engaged in different ways, yet our goal is the same—you. You are the cause of our service and also its final destination. You have so mercifully brought us into your movement and given us our real life’s work. We pray that you will not let us stray, always keeping us in your service.

Thank you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants at New Vraja Dhāma, Hungary.  
(written by Caitanya Candra dāsa)

## New Vraja (Khasadeśa)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the humble obeisances of your servants in New Vraja *dhāma,* Khasadeśa. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

It is only because of your mercy that the people of Khasadeśa can learn Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are like death-row prisoners who have been given a chance for redemption by carrying out a special wartime mission. This is our last chance for freedom: joining and pushing forward the *saṅkīrtana* movement. We are indebted to you forever. The best way to repay Your Divine Grace is to fulfill your mission.

*yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*  
*āmāra ājñāya guru hañā tāra’ ei deśa*

**“Instruct everyone to follow the orders of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as they are given in the** ***Bhagavad-gītā*** **and** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****. In this way become a spiritual master and try to liberate everyone in this land.”**

We cannot offer any public programs to celebrate your Centennial, but we promise that by the year 2096, everyone in Khasadeśa will have your books.

Please empower us to become true Prabhupādānugas.

Your grandsons and granddaughters in New Vraja, Khasadeśa.

## New Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances. All glories to you, the savior of the most fallen. We approach you as offenders to your lotus feet and beg for your causeless mercy. Please do not kick us away, for we have no other shelter.

The first thing that an aspiring disciple must learn is that he must follow the instructions of a bona fide spiritual master. There is no other process for making spiritual advancement. Concocting ideas on spiritual life, separate from the order of the spiritual master, will only lead to disaster, as we have experienced. There are different processes of learning. The first is to hear and follow. This is the best way and is recommended by the previous authorities in devotional service. The second process is to see another person’s mistakes and learn from them. This is second-class learning. The third process is to disobey and be punished for that disobedience. This is the learning process for fools. Unfortunately, we approach you from this third-class platform. Having deviated from your instructions on how to build New Vṛndāvana, we have nearly devastated the Brijabāsi mood of the spiritual world, which you established when you were present. Thus we have been offenders unto your lotus feet and unto the lotus feet of other Vaiṣṇavas. We beg your forgiveness, and we beg the forgiveness of the Vaiṣṇavas we offended by our actions. And we ask for another opportunity to sincerely serve your instructions on how to build New Vṛndāvana.

The holy *dhāma* descends by the mercy of the pure devotee. You have kindly requested the Supreme Lord to have His abode, Goloka Vṛndāvana, descend into the hills of West Virginia, a most unlikely place. This was your causeless mercy upon the Western world. Not until you placed your lotus feet on this land did the spiritual world descend. As the *Bhāgavatam* states:

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās*  
*tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni*  
*svāntaḥ-sthena gadā-bhṛtā*

**“My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.13.10).**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only because of your presence that this land in West Virginia became the spiritual world. No one else had that power, for we are only instruments in fulfilling your divine order. We have no qualifications of our own, having taken birth in the land of *mlecchas* and *yavanas*. This we have had to learn the hard way. From the top devotee down to the bottom, we can only be instruments in your hands, and if we decide otherwise, we will be cast aside as useless building material. One cannot concoct the process for building the holy *dhāma* under the guise of “Westernizing Krishna Consciousness.” Everything we need to know about how to build New Vṛndāvana is present in your instructions. We have seen that only those who want to fulfill your desire for building the *dhāma* are able to stay here. Others are forced to leave. Only by having implicit faith in the order of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa can one understand spiritual life.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy we are now waking up from our deviations and fixing our minds on your instructions to build seven temples on seven hills, to establish cow protection and a Rūpānuga Vidyāpīṭha for training *brāhmaṇas* and Vaiṣṇavas, and to institute self-sufficiency as a way of life. It was as if Dhenukāsura had invaded the hearts of the inhabitants of this holy place and made it difficult for anyone to come and eat the wonderful fruits here. Now that Lord Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma have kicked out our misconceptions, New Vṛndāvana is again inhabitable, and the Vaiṣṇavas are returning to taste the nectarean fruits of the holy *dhāma*. We are now confident that by following your instructions we will be successful. We have made great progress in the last year by just following the process you have established. Please continue to shower your blessings upon us, for we are most unqualified.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have kindly given us the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vṛndāvana-candra. The cooling rays of Their lotus feet are certainly the only shelter in this degraded Western culture. We want the whole world to come and experience the nectar of Their lotus feet. We are in the process of rebuilding and repopulating under your instructions. In our lifetime we hope to see these seven temples established, cow protection flourishing, preachers graduating from the Rūpānuga Vidyāpīṭha, and self-sufficiency as the way of life here. Please continue to bless us; otherwise we will sink into the Kali ocean of misconception and deviation.

Begging to remain under the shelter of your divine direction,  
Your devotees at New Vṛndāvana, West Virginia.

## New York

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most blessed, auspicious occasion of your divine appearance and Vyāsa-pūjā celebration!

For the sacrifice and austerities you performed in the course of delivering the seeds of *kṛṣṇa-prema* on behalf of the most munificent incarnations Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, we, your humble, menial servants, owe you an eternal debt. We understand your exalted worshipable position as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, but even more than that we know that you came directly from Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s paradise, Goloka, the supreme abode.

When we consider that you moved from the peaceful and serene atmosphere of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara Mandira in Vraja to a tiny dilapidated storefront in New York City’s Lower East Side, we appreciate deeply the sacrifice and austerity you endured for us.

But when we consider further that on Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s desire you came from Goloka to this world of misery to relieve all of us from our suffering condition and to deliver to us the matchless gift of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* we become awed and revere your deeds by offering unlimited obeisances and chanting your glories forever and a day.

On this special day we bathe in the dust of your lotus feet. We fall prostrate and offer Your Divine Grace our lives, and we pray to always receive your mercy and follow your footsteps back to Goloka Vṛndāvana *dhāma*.

Dear devotees, please understand for sure, today we joyously celebrate the appearance of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda’s confidential devotee. Śrīla Prabhupāda descended to distribute drops of Their loving mercy, and we will always revere His Divine Grace as our eternal father, the wonderful devotee whom all of us desire to love; we will hold in our hands his lotus feet, and he will take us back to our eternal home, Goloka, the abode of Lord Kṛṣṇa and His entourage of loving friends.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we ask you to be kind to us on this auspicious day and shower your mercy upon us as we pray,

*aṁśo bhagavato ’smy ahaṁ sadā dāso ’smi sarvathā*  
*tat-kṛpāpekṣako nityaṁ tat-preṣṭhā-sāt karomi svam*

**“I am a tiny part and parcel of the Supreme Lord, and I am always and in every way His eternal servant. As I am always hoping for His mercy and kindness, I offer myself to His most dear servitor, my spiritual master.”**

Your Divine Grace offered us the highest path, *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* to fulfill the goal of life, love of Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. Any one of your books can certainly bring us to the threshold of *kṛṣṇa-prema*. But if we don’t realize this fact, if we can’t realize what wisdom there is in these *ślokas* and purports, if we can’t go beyond licking the outside of the bottle of honey, it is not due to any shortcomings in what Kṛṣṇa told you to write but is due rather to our own lack of unflinching faith.

We can become free from the clutches of *māyā* only by taking shelter of service to your lotus feet. Any person who seriously desires to achieve life’s highest goal must seek out your guidance as his *śikṣā-guru* and take to heart the instructions in your transcendental books.

Even if we wander for many millions of years, from the creation of the cosmos until the time of annihilation, we cannot escape from the path of mundane activity unless we receive shelter at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are a pure devotee.

You have appeared in this Kali-yuga especially to fulfill the desires of the most munificent Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who delivers the topmost path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the unfortunate, fallen conditioned souls of this age.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is the transcendental gardener, and His pure devotees in the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava disciplic succession are delivering His harvest, the gift of *kṛṣṇa-prema*.

On this special day we bathe in the dust of your lotus feet, we fall prostrate and offer Your Divine Grace our lives, and we pray to always receive your mercy and follow your footsteps back to Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma.

Dear devotees, know for sure that His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda is a confidential associate of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and has come to personally assist Lord Gaurāṅga in distributing the sweetest, most precious fruit of love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

We therefore pray for your divine mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*he guro jñāna-da dīna-bandho*  
*svānanda-dātaḥ karuṇaika-sindho*  
*vṛndāvanāsīna-hitāvatāra*  
*prasīda rādhā-praṇaya-pracāra*

**“O spiritual master, O giver of divine wisdom, O friend of the fallen, you are the giver of your own bliss, and you are the only ocean of mercy. Although dwelling in Vṛndāvana, you have descended for the welfare of fallen souls like myself, and you are preaching the divine love of Rādhā for Kṛṣṇa. Please be kind upon me.”**

O savior of the unfortunate fallen souls, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you give in charity the greatest gift, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to anyone who is willing to accept your good fortune. You acted as the humble servant and master of all of us fortunate enough to have accepted your mercy.

You moved from the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara Mandira in Vṛndāvana to the Lower East Side of hell, but you brought with you the mood of Śrī Vṛndāvana and the predecessor *ācāryas,* and you revealed Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy and offered it for free to everyone by this Kṛṣṇa consciousness process of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, dancing in ecstasy, feasting on *kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* and preaching your message to the fallen conditioned souls.

Your Divine Grace came to 26 Second Avenue and transformed it into a corner of Vraja-maṇḍala. Throughout the world, by your *cintāmaṇi* touch you made Vṛndāvana available in places that are literally deserts and hells. You satisfied the hunger and thirst of our starving souls. You placed us on a strict diet of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, the ultimate medicine to relieve our distress and bring us true peace, happiness, and love.

On this most special day we bathe in the dust of your lotus feet, we fall prostate and offer Your Divine Grace our lives. And we pray to always receive your mercy and follow your footsteps back to Goloka Vṛndāvana-dhāma. Dear devotees, please know for sure that Śrīla Prabhupāda is your life and soul, your father and dearmost friend, and if you hold onto his lotus feet he will bring you home with him to dance and chant in the highest happiness in the Ocean of the Nectar of Devotion.

We pray to Your Divine Grace as insignificant, humble beggars,

*nāma-śreṣṭhaṁ manum api śacī-putram atra svarūpaṁ*  
*rūpaṁ tasyāgrajam uru-purīṁ māthurīṁ goṣṭhavātīm*  
*rādhā-kuṇḍaṁ giri-varam aho rādhikā-mādhavāśāṁ*  
*prāpto yasya prathita-kṛpaya śrī-guruṁ taṁ nato ’smi*

**“I bow down to the beautiful lotus feet of my spiritual master, by whose causeless mercy I have obtained the supreme holy name, the divine** ***mantra,*** **the service of the son of Śacīmātā, the association of Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara, Rūpa Gosvāmī, and Rūpa’s older brother Sanātana Gosvāmī, the supreme abode of Mathurā, the blissful abode of Vṛndāvana, the divine Rādhā-kuṇḍa and Govardhana Hill, and the desire within my heart for the loving service of Śrī Rādhikā and Mādhava in Vṛndāvana.”**

Praying at the lotus feet of His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, always desiring his mercy, we beg to remain his servants forever.

Your eternal servants from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira Brooklyn, New York.  
(written by Rāmabhadra dāsa)

## Nicaragua

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please be so kind as to accept our most respectful and humble obeisances. All glories to you! All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā, the highest transcendental event in the three worlds. The world without your mercy is unimaginable. In a flash you have transformed the face of the earth. All who have had the honor of knowing you in one way or another are smiling now. The sparkle of love shines from the eyes of your followers. The anxiety to serve moves the heart of your devotees. The urge of forgiveness predominates in the deepest feelings of your representatives. To sing, dance, and distribute *prasādam* and holy literature have become the most noble activities of your followers and the followers of your followers. So your divine plan to awaken and purify sleeping humanity is little by little becoming a reality.

Where can one not hear of Kṛṣṇa? In which corner of the world have your books not been distributed? So much accomplished in so little time!! Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, could have made Satya-yuga inside Kali-yuga. Such a change would be impossible without your knowledge, strength, purity, and surrender. It is only by your example that your followers are so inspired and motivated. The planet is being illuminated by your teachings, and it is because of you that Kṛṣṇa is known worldwide.

O Patita-pāvana! Permit us to glorify this event again and again, submerged in the deep feeling of thanksgiving that spontaneously arises from the deepest heart of hearts of those fortunate souls who have had the mercy of being saved by your infinite kindness. How could there ever be a way to repay you for all the mercy we have received from you?

All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories!!

Your servants in Nicaragua.  
(written by Parameśvara dāsa)

## Nova Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Here you are in Nova Gokula Dhāma,  
Looking gravely at us from the *vyāsāsana.*  
In our hearts you are, cleaning our dirts,  
In our minds you are, enlightening it with your purports.  
Everywhere you are, pervading this *saṅkīrtana* movement.  
In our farm community you are,  
Establishing this *varṇāśrama* system.  
In every corner you are, spreading the holy name.  
And we are following behind, trying to spread your fame.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Here we are, looking at you ashamed,  
Preaching although we are dumb, jumping although we are lame.  
Here we are, Kali-yuga fallen souls,  
Trying to serve you nicely, although we are low.

Your servants in Nova Gokula, Pindamonhangaba, Sao Paulo, Brazil.  
(written by Paravyoma dāsa Adhikārī)

## Nueva Goloka Vṛndāvana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. This ISKCON movement you have created is like a transcendental hospital, saving all humanity from the horrible epidemic provoked by the materialistic way of life. This epidemic kills any devotional sentiment and gives rise to material self-interest on the pretext of time, place, and circumstance.

But you appear in the shape of your books, and with cutting words you extirpate the illusion of those who open their pages. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the intelligence to understand your instructions properly and the determination to put them into practice. Remove our rascaldom, which leads us to cover ourselves with so-called learning—well-learned arguments meant not to help others but to eliminate the necessity of becoming pure.

You came to Costa Rica in the shape of your books, your music, your classes, your *mūrti* installed in the temple, your disciples, the disciples of your disciples, etc. So, you are saving us from this epidemic! If you aren’t the person who is saving us from the infection of *māyā,* who would it be?

Recently we began many inspired projects on this farm, which will occupy all your devotees in full Kṛṣṇa consciousness. On this most blessed day of your appearance, we ask you to help us to never lose sight of the objective, to help us extricate ourselves from the labyrinth of selfish desires, to help us avoid turning this hopeful, fledgling Vaiṣṇava community into a heap of isolated plans for personal happiness.

Your Centennial is now the center of our activities, including the construction of a new temple in the middle of the farm, which will be the core of Pueblo Hare Krishna (Hare Krishna Village); the construction of the first houses for the devotees now dwelling in the city, who are ready to emigrate to this program of “simple living and high thinking”; the construction of a *gurukula,* which will assemble all your devotee children and save them from learning atheism and degradation in *karmī* schools; agricultural projects, in which some *gṛhasthas* will combine their efforts to realize your wishes concerning self-sufficiency; and, above all, the uninterrupted distribution of your books, the only salvation for humanity, by which they can find the exit from material entanglement.

Every devotee of the community is promising to study your books more conscientiously, to memorize more *Bhagavad-gītā* verses, to fix a goal of distributing a certain number of books per month, to improve poor *sādhana,* etc. In short, your Centennial has become a brilliant inspiration helping us escape from the net of *māyā*.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, what would the devotees do without you?

Your servants in Nueva Goloka Vṛndāvana, Costa Rica.

## Ottawa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, with the Centennial anniversary of your glorious appearance quickly approaching, your servants everywhere are preparing special offerings to honor and glorify you. Many significant projects are targeted for completion, as well as other programs such as *padayātrā* in one hundred countries, and others in multiples of one hundred.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have instructed us that “Purity is the force,” “Preaching is the essence,” “Books are the basis,” and “Utility is the principle.” If we all dedicate and rededicate ourselves to chanting our minimum sixteen rounds of the *mahā-mantra* every day and following the four regulative principles, this will keep us pure and will be the force to attract more and more conditioned souls to your lotus feet. If we continue to preach as vigorously as you did, using your books and instructions as the basis, with emphasis on the distribution of the three transcendental products—the Lord’s transcendental holy name, His transcendental literatures, and His transcendental *prasādam*—you and your Lord will supply everything for His mission to continue for the rest of the Golden Era. And if we utilize everything in the service of you and your Lord while maintaining a humble, cooperative and nonpartisan attitude, the continued growth and success of your ISKCON is guaranteed.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have said that the only danger to your ISKCON is from within; nothing from outside can hamper or destroy your ISKCON. Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we are all endeavoring to offer something special in your honor, we are still dependent on your causeless mercy. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to grant us the proper desire, intelligence, and spiritual strength to remain faithful to you.

Your aspiring servants at the ISKCON temple in Ottawa, Canada.  
(written by Śaṅkara dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your appearance. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Pandharpur is the most celebrated holy place of central India and the spiritual heart of Maharashtra. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* describes its glorious importance as a place of pilgrimage. Lord Kṛṣṇa resides here in His Deity form known as Lord Viṭṭhala. Lakhs of people come here from all over India to see this unique black Deity of the Lord, with arms gathered at His waist, waiting for His devotees to come to Him.

Pandharpur has been visited by many spiritual leaders, including Śaṅkarācārya and Vallabhācārya. Lord Caitanya Himself went there to take *darśana* of Lord Viṭṭhala on His tour of South India. In fact, it is in this very same city that Lord Caitanya discovered that His elder brother Viśvarūpa had achieved *samādhi*. Thus Pandharpur was blessed by the personal presence of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Tukārāma, the celebrated saint of Maharashtra, used to visit this place very frequently to get the *darśana* of his worshipable Lord Viṭṭhala. Pandharpur was also the preaching headquarters of many saints of Maharashtra in medieval times. In order to make spiritual progress, thousands of people flock here to see Lord Viṭṭhala. Every pilgrim gets the special opportunity of touching the feet of the Lord.

By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have a small ISKCON center here in Pandharpur, situated on the charming bank of the river Chandrabhaga. A dozen devotees are engaged in spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. By Lord Caitanya’s causeless mercy, your movement is very much appreciated in this area. The way local people chant the holy name and glorify the Lord very much resembles the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas’ *harināma-saṅkīrtana*.

By the causeless mercy of Lord Kṛṣṇa, we have already got enough land to begin building our ISKCON Pandharpur Project. We are planning to start the project right after your Centennial celebration in 1996.

But, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it will be possible only by your mercy. We are qualified neither materially nor spiritually. We seek your mercy and guidance at every single step. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us so that we may be valuable instruments in your hands for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, at least in this tract of land. We owe you all our good fortune.

Your insignificant servants of ISKCON Pandharpur, India.

## Parati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You came to this material world because of your mercy toward the fallen souls, to bring perfect spiritual knowledge so that we can free ourselves from the concept of material life.

Through your learned and conclusive instructions you have been teaching us the direct and easiest way to recover our true nature, which is Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are souls eternally indebted to you and dependent on your constant mercy, because at every step *māyā* gets stronger, causing illusion in this hard material nature.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we are unqualified to serve you, we have been trying to fulfill your desire to establish a self-sufficient community, dependent on Kṛṣṇa and based on the concept of simple living and high thinking.

Dear Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow us to remain under your shelter, in the shade of your lotus feet.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our eternal well-wisher!

Your servants in Gaura Vṛndāvana dhāma, Parati, Brazil.

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

Taking this opportunity of the celebration of your transcendental appearance, we would like to recall a few instructions you gave the last time you came to Paris. It was in 1976; the temple was at 4 rue Lesueur. You said, “Lord Viṣṇu and His potency are one. But there is a third energy, this material world, which is a reflection of the transcendental energy. We all have to work to achieve anything in this reflection, whereas in the spiritual world everything is supplied automatically. So the more you spiritualize the whole atmosphere, the more you become sufficient and happy.”

Then you encouraged the devotees to continue sticking to the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and engaging their energies somehow in practical service to Kṛṣṇa. You assured them that by doing so they would never lack anything and would always remain happy. Finally you concluded, “I think this is the fourth time I have come here? So I find you very happy. Thank you very much.”

Thank you thousands of times, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us such a simple formula to make our life happy. Often you would also challenge your disciples by saying, “What is the difficulty? There is no difficulty!”

Today almost twenty years have passed since your last visit to the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara temple. The difficulty is that we are not serious enough to actually do as you say. Pulled away by egoistic desires, we lack the vision and power necessary to fulfill your transcendental instructions. Of course, we can take a positive view and say that things are getting better each year in the Paris temple. Book distribution is increasing steadily, your program of *prasādam* distribution is manifesting more and more, and everybody who happens to visit the Paris temple is impressed by the standard of worship offered to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara.

Still, Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can we be satisfied with such limited achievements? Nineteen-ninety-six is coming very fast. Only 365 days are separating us from the celebration of your hundredth anniversary; the Centennial is becoming a reality. Now the time has come for us to make a very sincere endeavor to come closer to the platform of pure devotional service that you always exemplified so perfectly.

How can we sit idly while so many people ignorant of the spiritual reality are rushing toward hell, victims of Kali-yuga? How can we say “Everything is fine” when for most of the people of France our public image is still that of a dangerous cult? How can we feel satisfied when your beloved Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara do not even have a proper house to stay in? And most of all, how can we be really happy when so many of your dear devotees are missing in this assembly today and cannot share their memories and realizations with us?

But today is not a day for lamentation, for on this most blessed occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā we can feel more than ever how we are just like insignificant glowworms in front of you. We therefore appeal for your merciful glance upon us and beg for your mercy to keep us engaged in this wonderful movement. Sometimes a small child wants to please his father by offering him some gift. But because the child completely depends upon his father for everything, the father himself gives him the money and instructions how to purchase the gift. In the same way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that you please bless us with all intelligence, strength, and sincerity so that we can make a grand success of your Centennial.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this wonderful Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*! Śrīla Prabhupāda Centennial*-kī jaya*!

Your humble servants in Paris, France.

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who have introduced to the Western world the purest form of religion, in the form of the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

You have given the fallen souls a quick and efficient elevator to the spiritual sky.

A bona fide guru is considered the representative of Vyāsa, and the anniversary of his appearance day is thus called Vyāsa-pūjā, or “worship of Vyāsa.”

As the most empowered successor of Śrīla Vyāsadeva in modern times, you showed us the real example of an *ācārya* by strictly following the scriptural injunctions, energetically preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and pushing your followers to do the same.

You are the perfect example of complete dedication to the mission of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa. Thus whenever we remember Your Divine Grace, especially on this anniversary day, we experience how this very remembrance permits us to transcend all the differences that may arise among ourselves during the normal course of activities.

Your example serves as an inspiration for us at all times, in all places, and in all circumstances. This is especially true now that we have Hari-Śauri Prabhu’s *A Transcendental Diary.*

For example, some of us are beginning to feel the incurable and irreversible attack of old age. Does that mean we should retire from active engagement in your ISKCON Society? That is not the example you yourself set. On the contrary, you showed an indomitable determination to fight for Kṛṣṇa up to the last breath. “I simply want the benediction like Arjuna, to fight for Kṛṣṇa to the last breath.” This was your answer to your many disciples who tried to dissuade you from traveling to London in July 1976, when you were afflicted with a heavy cold.

May Kṛṣṇa bless us with a similar unyielding determination, even though our service may be insignificant.

In a letter to Upendra dāsa in September 1972, you outlined your expectations for Perth: “I shall expect the Perth center to be first class in every way.” And we can see how your energies are working wonderfully, even in this most isolated city.

Many serious candidates are taking to the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra*. This is the proof that for those who really want it, Kṛṣṇa consciousness is there, unspoiled, as you personally delivered it. All the advancement that has been made in our preaching field is ultimately to your credit.

You are the actual father, because in the form of your books you have given us the instruction manuals on how to act wisely in this material world and how to ultimately leave it behind.

*Padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām.* There is danger at every step, and as Kali-yuga progresses, the conditions of life will become more and more abominable.
